**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A tenant that ended up with no subscription (a lost or dead-lettered event) gets its trial within the hour, automatically.
- [ ] If the provider or Ikaro's own reconcile has been down for a day, no paying tenant is suspended; the freeze lifts by itself.
- [ ] A paused provider webhook is re-enabled by the system where the provider allows it, and the alert stays raised until a webhook delivery succeeds after the re-enable (a cleared flag is not recovery, and a wrong token keeps failing with `401`); otherwise an alert names the runbook.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit:
  - [ ] Each step in isolation, including: tenant younger than the window skipped; repair idempotent; freeze set at 24 h without a reconcile success and cleared after one; step failure isolation (one step failing does not stop the others)
- Integration:
  - [ ] End to end on the fake: delete a subscription row, run the job, assert the trial is recreated with the tenant's country / timezone
  - [ ] With the freeze active, S06's sweep suspends nobody; after a reconcile success the freeze clears
  - [ ] `listTenantsCreatedBefore` returns exactly the documented fields (`tenantId`, `countryCode`, `timezone`, `slug`, `createdAt`, `isActive`) and nothing else, never joins `billing` tables, and its query uses the `created_at` standalone index (`EXPLAIN` asserted); a registration `RESERVED` for more than 1 hour is released, a younger one and a `COMPLETED` one are not (boundary at exactly 1 hour)
- Tenant isolation: [ ] the repair acts per tenant id; the port returns one tenant's fields only
- E2E: none — covered by integration; live check above
- [ ] `architecture-check` passes (no new cross-context edge direction: only the existing Billing → Platform entry gains one target path)
- [ ] The `AddTenantsCreatedAtIndex` migration is index-only (a plain `CREATE INDEX` that runs under `migrationsTransactionMode "all"`; no column or constraint change) and the `health_read_failures` migration adds one defaulted column — M26 adds no breaking migration
- [ ] Keyset paging: with a page size smaller than the tenant count, one run reaches a subscription-less tenant that sits beyond the first page; inactive tenants are skipped
- [ ] No `provider_health` row → not frozen; freeze flap-proof rule (set at 24 h or two failed health reads, cleared only after a real reconcile success and a successful health read)
- [ ] The two-failed-reads rule: one failed read leaves `health_read_failures = 1` and no freeze, a second consecutive one sets the freeze, a successful read in between resets the counter to 0; set and clear never violate `CHK_billing_provider_health_freeze` (`freeze_active` and `freeze_since` change together)
- [ ] The re-enable alert closes only when a processed `provider_events` row newer than the re-enable exists; a cleared provider flag alone, or only an unprocessed / older row, leaves it raised
- [ ] The repair of a subscription-less tenant calls `CreateTrialSubscriptionUseCase` with no `adminEmail`, `registrationId` or `eventId` and `occurredAt` = the injected clock's now (fake clock asserted); the resulting trial has 14 full days and no billing email
- [ ] The release sweep deletes `RESERVED` rows older than 1 h and nothing else; the route is annotated (`@NoFeatureGate()` + explicit `@SuspensionAccess(...)`)
- [ ] Checkov clean
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S09 — Dunning reminders and the `cron-billing-reminders` trigger (UC-139)

**Agent:** `backend-ts + devops`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-139 (all of it), `docs/DOMAIN_EVENTS.md` § BillingReminderDue, `docs/DOMAIN_MODEL.md` § Aggregate: Subscription (`plan_confirmed_at`), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_BACKEND.md` § Billing (partly built), `infra/terraform/README.md` § New-resource PR-sequencing playbook (cron trigger row)
**Dependencies:** M26-S02 (charge selectors), M26-S03 (`BillingReminderDue` class), M26-S05 (trigger constants and `CronBillingController`), M25-S16 / M25-S20 (gating decorators on the cron route), M26-S06 (the `ProviderHealth` repository, read for the day-12 reminder)
**Pattern:** Trigger handler → job use case emitting a `BillingReminderDue` **Command per milestone** through the outbox, deduplicated by its business `dedup_key`; consumed by S12 (M26-D6; the command's topic is created by S12's `subscribe()`). Its own standalone-index selection with no operational-status filter (overdue reminders serve `PAST_DUE` and recovery-charge tenants).

**Description:**
Warn the manager repeatedly, with dates, before anything is suspended, with no human sending reminders. Daily trigger `cron-billing-reminders` (`0 11 * * *`, 08:00 in São Paulo, M26-D5; manual publisher `POST /cron/billing-reminders`). For each non-exempt subscription the job publishes `BillingReminderDue` **once per milestone**, deduplicated by `(subscription, kind, daysOffset, chargeId)`: `PLAN_CONFIRMATION` at 10, 7 and 4 days before trial end while `plan_confirmed_at` is NULL (the last says the default plan will be invoiced in one day); `TRIAL_ENDING` at 7 and 3 days before trial end and on the last day (with the payment link when the first invoice exists); `CHARGE_DUE_SOON` 3 days before a charge's due date (**not** for the first trial charge — `TRIAL_ENDING` covers it the same day — and **not** for a `RECOVERY` charge — `RECOVERY_CHARGE_OPEN` covers it); `CHARGE_OVERDUE` at 1, 3, 7 and 12 days after the due date of a `PAST_DUE` subscription (the last states the suspension date); `RECOVERY_CHARGE_OPEN` for a `SUSPENDED` subscription (reasons `NON_PAYMENT` and `PAYMENT_REVERSED` only — not `CANCELED` or `OPERATOR`) whose `RECOVERY` charge is still open: 3 days after the charge is issued, and at 1 and 7 days after its due date once it is `OVERDUE` (the email says the account stays suspended until the one-month charge is paid and links the payment page; it does not quote the clean-up date, because no job acts on it before M27); it stops when the charge is paid or cancelled. Without it a suspended tenant that ignored the first recovery email would never hear from Ikaro again. Ikaro sends every reminder itself because the provider's customer notifications are off. **Deduplication needs no new column or table:** the milestone key `(subscription, kind, daysOffset, chargeId)` is the `dedup_key` of the `BillingReminderDue` **Command** in `shared.outbox` (S03; the same mechanism as `BookingReminderDue`), whose `UNIQUE` makes a second publication of the same milestone a no-op. **Freshness is Billing's job, not Notification's (R3):** Pub/Sub is at-least-once with no delivery-time guarantee and Notification has no Billing read port, so the job re-checks **immediately before writing the outbox row** that the charge is still open (`PENDING` / `OVERDUE`) and that the subscription is not `exempt`; it never publishes a command for an exempt subscription or for a paid or cancelled charge (A1). A small stale window between publish and send is accepted, and the reminder copy is tolerant ("Se você já pagou, desconsidere este aviso"). No recipient → logged, the dashboard banner remains (A2). The command carries the nullable `recipientEmail` that Billing resolved (S03); S12 falls back to the managers when it is null. **Dates:** the `CHARGE_OVERDUE` day-12 reminder states the suspension date computed from the sweep's own rule (`grace_until` when set, else `past_due_since` + 14 days) in the tenant timezone, and is not sent while the suspension freeze is active for the subscription's merchant key (a frozen provider suspends nobody, so a "you will be suspended on …" warning would be false; `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-139 is silent on this and is aligned to it). Milestones match the **exact** `daysOffset`; a missed cron day loses that milestone permanently (accepted and stated). "Business days" anywhere in the reminder rules means Mon-Fri with no holiday calendar in M26 (known limitation).

**Devops half (cron trigger).** **PR sequence:** as S05 — 1 PR + 1 Foundation apply (PR1, `infra-app-mix-ok`: the `registerTrigger()` call site, `pubsub-catalog.json` regenerated, the `ikaro-cron-billing-reminders` entry in `modules/scheduler`'s `locals.jobs` plus both scheduler test fixtures and the `variables.tf` text as S05 (no per-topic `envs/*` edit apart from the pause below; no Foundation edit, S05's catalog-derived publisher grant covers it); then the manual Foundation apply); schedule daily, created paused in production (S39 enables it). **The job is paused in staging too until S12 lands (Decided at the M26 audit (2026-11-18)):** `BillingReminderDue` is a Command with no audit-log line and **no topic until S12's `subscribe()` call site creates one**, so a running job would write outbox rows the relay cannot publish; this PR therefore also adds `cron-billing-reminders` to `paused_job_keys` in `infra/terraform/envs/staging/main.tf`, and S12's PR removes it. **Live-verification check:** `get-iam-policy` populated; `gcloud scheduler jobs run` is not used on staging (the job is paused); instead call the manual publisher locally or run the integration test, and with a subscription 7 days from trial end observe the `billing_reminders_run` log line and one `shared.outbox` row whose `dedup_key` is the `(subscription, kind, daysOffset, chargeId)` milestone key (a Command has no audit-log line, S03). Pass = IAM policy populated + the log line + the outbox row; delivery to a consumer is verified by S12.

**Backend use case steps:**
1. **`PublishBillingRemindersUseCase`** (job), per-subscription milestone evaluation as a pure domain service `ReminderSchedule` (testable without a database).
**Backend HTTP surface:** extends `cron-billing.controller.ts` — `POST /cron/billing-reminders` (publisher only).
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** none (deduplication is the outbox `dedup_key`).

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/domain/reminder-schedule.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`) (new)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/use-cases/publish-billing-reminders.use-case.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts`) (new)
- `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-139 (modify — state that the day-12 `CHARGE_OVERDUE` reminder is not sent while the suspension freeze is active; UC-139, `docs/BOUNDED_CONTEXTS.md` and the `BillingReminderDue` entry in DOMAIN_EVENTS already carry all five reminder kinds)
- `infra/terraform/envs/staging/main.tf` (modify — `paused_job_keys` gains `cron-billing-reminders` until S12)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/events/reminders-trigger.handler.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`) (new — class `RemindersTriggerHandler`, consumer name in `cron-trigger-names.constants.ts`)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/events/cron-trigger-names.constants.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/cron-billing.controller.ts` (+ specs), `apps/backend/http/billing/cron.http` (modify)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/billing.module.ts` (modify)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/ports/subscription-repository.port.ts`, `charge-repository.port.ts`, their TypeORM adapters and in-memory doubles (modify — the `…ForJob` reminder selectors: trialing by `trial_ends_at`, open charges by due date, `PAST_DUE`, `SUSPENDED` with open recovery; each with an `EXPLAIN` integration test), and `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/repositories/caching-subscription.repository.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`) (modify — forward each new subscription selector uncached)
- `infra/terraform/modules/scheduler/main.tf`, `infra/terraform/modules/scheduler/variables.tf`, `infra/terraform/modules/scheduler/tests/resources_unit_test.tftest.hcl`, `infra/terraform/modules/scheduler/tests/variables_unit_test.tftest.hcl`, `infra/terraform/pubsub-catalog.json` (modify)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A trial tenant gets plan-confirmation reminders at 10, 7 and 4 days, trial-ending warnings at 7 and 3 days and on the last day; a due charge gets a reminder 3 days before; an overdue one at 1, 3, 7 and 12 days (the last names the suspension date) — each exactly once.
- [ ] Nobody is reminded about a charge they already paid (Billing re-checks at publish time; a payment landing in the short window before the email is read is covered by the tolerant copy).
- [ ] The first trial charge gets `TRIAL_ENDING` only (no same-day `CHARGE_DUE_SOON`), and a recovery charge gets `RECOVERY_CHARGE_OPEN` only.
- [ ] A suspended tenant (non-payment or reversed payment) with an open recovery charge is reminded 3 days after issue and at 1 and 7 days after its due date, each once; nothing is sent for a `CANCELED` or `OPERATOR` suspension.
- [ ] Exempt tenants receive nothing.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit:
  - [ ] `ReminderSchedule` table-driven over every milestone and boundary (UTC-3 and DST tenants), including "plan confirmed → no `PLAN_CONFIRMATION`"
  - [ ] Exempt skipped; handler calls exactly one use case and passes `correlationId`
  - [ ] Publish-time re-check: a charge paid or cancelled, or a subscription turned exempt, between selection and the outbox write publishes nothing; the day-12 reminder's suspension date honours `grace_until`
  - [ ] `CHARGE_DUE_SOON` is not produced for the first trial charge nor for a `RECOVERY` charge; a skipped cron day does not back-fill the missed milestone
- Integration:
  - [ ] Running the job twice on one day publishes each milestone once (the outbox `dedup_key` holds under concurrency)
  - [ ] End to end through the in-memory bus on the fake clock: advance time day by day and assert the exact sequence of commands for one trial and one overdue subscription
  - [ ] Selection uses standalone indexes (`EXPLAIN`)
- Tenant isolation: [ ] reminders carry their own `tenantId`; one run covers several tenants without crossing data
  - [ ] Freeze active for the subscription's merchant key → the day-12 `CHARGE_OVERDUE` reminder is not published, the day-1, 3 and 7 ones still are; a later run with the freeze cleared does not back-fill day 12 (exact `daysOffset` matching)
- E2E: none — covered by integration; live check above
- [ ] Checkov clean
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S10 — `provider_events` retention purge and the `cron-billing-provider-events-purge` trigger (UC-114)

**Agent:** `backend-ts + devops`
**Complexity:** S
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-114 (the retention job row), `docs/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md` § `billing.provider_events`, `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing cron triggers, `docs/DATA_RETENTION_AND_PRIVACY.md` (retention conventions), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_TESTING.md` § Standalone index for a cross-tenant system job, `infra/terraform/README.md` § New-resource PR-sequencing playbook (cron trigger row)
**Dependencies:** M26-S02 (the `ProviderEvent` model, port and repository this story extends), M26-S04 (the table is populated), M26-S05 (trigger constants and `CronBillingController`), M25-S16 / M25-S20 (gating decorators on the cron route)
**Pattern:** Trigger handler → retention use case with a bounded batch delete (precedent: `LeadFormRetentionPurgeTriggerHandler`).

**Description:**
Keep the webhook inbox small. Daily trigger `cron-billing-provider-events-purge` (`30 5 * * *`, M26-D5; manual publisher `POST /cron/billing-provider-events-purge`) deletes `provider_events` older than 30 days in bounded batches through the standalone index on `received_at` (created in M25-S03). `PurgeProviderEventsUseCase` takes no tenant (the table is processed globally; each row carries its resolved `tenant_id` and event type but no payload or personal data), deletes in batches of a fixed size and stops at a per-run limit. Idempotent by nature. Postgres `DELETE` has no `LIMIT`, so each batch is `DELETE FROM billing.provider_events WHERE (provider, event_id) IN (SELECT provider, event_id FROM billing.provider_events WHERE received_at < $1 ORDER BY received_at LIMIT $2)` (the inner select uses the `received_at` index). (The 30-day figure is the canonical retention; the provider's own 14-day expiry is why reconcile exists.)

**Devops half (cron trigger).** **PR sequence:** as S05 — 1 PR + 1 Foundation apply (PR1, `infra-app-mix-ok`: the `registerTrigger()` call site, `pubsub-catalog.json` regenerated, the `ikaro-cron-billing-provider-events-purge` entry in `modules/scheduler`'s `locals.jobs` plus both scheduler test fixtures and the `variables.tf` text as S05 (no per-topic `envs/*` edit, no Foundation edit: S05's catalog-derived publisher grant covers it); then the manual Foundation apply); schedule daily, created paused in production (S39 enables it). **Live-verification check:** `get-iam-policy` populated and one manual `gcloud scheduler jobs run ikaro-cron-billing-provider-events-purge` observed as the `billing_provider_events_purge_run` log line (rows deleted, batches, per-run limit hit or not). Pass = both observed.

**Backend use case steps:**
1. **`PurgeProviderEventsUseCase`**.
**Backend HTTP surface:** extends `cron-billing.controller.ts` — `POST /cron/billing-provider-events-purge` (publisher only; `@NoFeatureGate()` plus an explicit `@SuspensionAccess(...)`).
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** none.

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/use-cases/purge-provider-events.use-case.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts`) (new)
- `docs/DATA_RETENTION_AND_PRIVACY.md` (modify — the "Planned: Billing data retention" section already exists with the `provider_events` 30-day row; flip that row from planned to implemented. The fiscal-retention years stay open until the accountant answers, S39 records them; S24 adds the signup and billing-account personal data)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/events/provider-events-purge-trigger.handler.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`) (new — class `ProviderEventsPurgeTriggerHandler`, consumer name constant in `cron-trigger-names.constants.ts`)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/events/cron-trigger-names.constants.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/cron-billing.controller.ts` (+ specs), `apps/backend/http/billing/cron.http` (modify)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/ports/provider-event-repository.port.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/repositories/typeorm-provider-event.repository.ts` (+ specs) and the in-memory provider-event repository double (modify — `deleteOlderThanBatch`)

### M26-S11 — Notification: platform-scoped template lookup and the Billing templates (UC-124 precondition)

**Agent:** `backend-ts`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-124 (preconditions: no per-tenant copy, no backfill), `docs/BOUNDED_CONTEXTS.md` § 3. Notification Context and § Communication Patterns (the event-to-email map), `docs/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md` § Schema: `notification` (`notification_templates`, `tenant_id` nullability), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_BACKEND.md` § Adding a new notification type (steps 1–7), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_SHARED.md` § Authoring new i18n UI copy keys, `docs/ANTI_PATTERNS.md` (plain `ALTER TABLE`)
**Dependencies:** M26-S03 (the event and command classes whose names the 17 `eventName` values below must equal); none on the notification gate (the story planned as M25-S23 was dropped: the Billing email handlers declare `skipWhenSuspended: false`, S12, so these templates need no exemption flag)
**Pattern:** The existing five-file notification-type recipe (enum → mapping → both `notifications.json` → migration → send use case) plus **one explicit key family**: `PLATFORM_SCOPED_TEMPLATE_KEYS`, a domain constant listing the Billing keys. The family is the least machinery that keeps platform rows out of the per-tenant default copy and is **flagged as a judgment call for story discovery**.

**Description:**
Billing emails are platform-level — UC-124: "no per-tenant copy and no backfill". **A trap the earlier draft missed:** in Notification, rows with `tenant_id IS NULL` are the *global defaults*, and `copyGlobalDefaultsForTenant` (called when a tenant is provisioned, and by `SeedDefaultTemplatesUseCase` / `seed.ts`) copies **every** `tenant_id IS NULL` row for the locale into the new tenant. Seeding the Billing templates as plain NULL rows would copy them into every tenant. The mechanism, with no schema change: (1) add the Billing keys to `NotificationTemplateKey` (`notification-template-key.enum.ts`) and their entries to `notification-template-key.mapping.ts` — **each entry is `{eventName, recipientType, variables, platformScoped: true}`**: `variables` is the placeholder list the mapping spec compares with `notifications.json` in both languages (docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_BACKEND.md "every placeholder in a template must be supplied"), and `platformScoped` marks the family (the flag is added to the mapping's entry type in this story; there is no suspension-gate flag, because the Billing handlers' own `skipWhenSuspended: false`, S12, lets these emails through while the tenant is suspended). **One template key per wording** (docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_BACKEND.md "wording that varies by actor or reason is a separate template key, not a sentence variable"; the localization catalog holds whole `{subject, body}` templates addressed by `{eventName}.{recipientType}`, so a `kind`-style variable would hard-code copy in code — M23-S28 reversed exactly that). The keys (the enum value is the kebab-case of the name; `eventName.recipientType` in `notifications.json`): `CHARGE_ISSUED_PERIOD`, `CHARGE_ISSUED_RECOVERY`, `CHARGE_PAID`, `SUBSCRIPTION_PAST_DUE`, `SUBSCRIPTION_SUSPENDED_NON_PAYMENT` (reasons `NON_PAYMENT` and `PAYMENT_REVERSED`), `SUBSCRIPTION_SUSPENDED_OPERATOR` (no payment instructions, "contact Ikaro"), `SUBSCRIPTION_REACTIVATED`, `SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED_IMMEDIATE`, `SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED_SCHEDULED`, `PLAN_CHANGED`, `BILLING_REMINDER_PLAN_CONFIRMATION`, `BILLING_REMINDER_TRIAL_ENDING`, `BILLING_REMINDER_CHARGE_DUE_SOON`, `BILLING_REMINDER_CHARGE_OVERDUE`, `BILLING_REMINDER_RECOVERY_CHARGE_OPEN`, `SUBSCRIPTION_PRICE_CHANGE_ANNOUNCED`, and (reserved, shipped now so M27 needs no template migration) `TENANT_PURGE_WARNING` — **17 keys**. There is no key for `SubscriptionSuspended` with reason `CANCELED`: the manager gets the `SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED_*` email for a cancel and S12 sends nothing for that suspension reason. S12 selects the key from event data (`kind`, `reason`, `immediate`, `ChargeIssued.kind`). The `ChargeIssued` trial-fallback email states only the total and due date (the event carries no line items); (2) the domain constant `PLATFORM_SCOPED_TEMPLATE_KEYS` is **derived from the `platformScoped` flag of the mapping** (`Object.entries(NOTIFICATION_TEMPLATE_KEY_MAPPING).filter(...)`), never a second hand-kept list; (3) `copyGlobalDefaultsForTenant` (the repository method, called only by `SeedDefaultTemplatesUseCase`) excludes the platform-scoped trigger events (`AND trigger_event <> ALL($3)`), so no tenant ever receives a copy — a unit/integration test proves it; **`apps/backend/src/shared/database/seed.ts` does not call that method**: it has two raw `INSERT … SELECT` copies of every `tenant_id IS NULL` row (the functions `seedNotificationTemplates` and `seedTeamTenant`) which get the same exclusion, so the E2E seed tenants never receive Billing rows (the keys come from the notification domain constant, or the seed lists the excluded `trigger_event` values — `shared/database` may not import a context domain file without an architecture-policy exception, so use the explicit list there with a spec that compares it to the constant); (4) a new, explicit repository lookup `findPlatformTemplate(key, locale)` reads only `tenant_id IS NULL AND trigger_event = ANY(platform-scoped)`. **Decided at the M26 audit (2026-11-18): the platform row is an existence-and-trigger-key row, not a text source.** `BaseNotificationUseCase.localizeTemplates` overlays every fetched template's subject and body from `notifications.json` (keyed by the mapping's `eventName` / `recipientType`) before rendering, so the row's own text is never used (`base-notification.use-case.ts`, "only its triggerEvent/channel/existence matter"); the seeded row's subject/body are therefore placeholders that the migration copies from `notifications.json` for readability only, and `findPlatformTemplate` exists to prove the key is provisioned (a missing row is the UC-124 A1 failure below). Why: one source of copy for every email, no second place for a wording to drift. The existing tenant-strict `findAllByTriggerEvent(tenantId, …)` is unchanged (it returns `[]` for an unknown tenant template and never falls back to the `tenant_id IS NULL` row), so no existing send path can fall back to a global template by accident; (5) copy in **both** `packages/i18n/locales/pt-BR/notifications.json` and `…/en/notifications.json` (the migration\'s `buildSeedRows()` throws if either locale lacks a key) — a complete, shippable sentence set in both languages (never `TODO`); (6) one data migration inserts the platform rows (idempotent via `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` on `UQ_notification_templates_global`; on a brand-new database `1748100000010-CreateNotificationTemplates` already seeds every enum value, so this migration is then a no-op), registered in `integration-global-setup.ts` and listed in `PERSISTENCE_BYPASS_IGNORES` (`apps/backend/eslint.config.js`). A missing platform template at send time fails loudly (UC-124 A1): a structured error log carrying the fixed field `event: "billing_template_missing"` (a log-based metric, which S36's alert policy reads; no app-defined OTel instrument, `docs/OBSERVABILITY_STRATEGY.md`) — decided: yes, an alert, because billing emails are money-critical and a template can be absent at runtime (`docs/OBSERVABILITY_STRATEGY.md` § 7. Dashboards, alerts and uptime checks (it already lists the policy)) — never a silent drop. Every Billing email handler declares `skipWhenSuspended: false` (S12): that declaration, not the template, is what lets the email through while the tenant is suspended.

**The 17 keys, tied to S03 (`eventName.recipientType` in `notifications.json` = the mapping entry's `eventName` / `recipientType`; every `eventName` is an S03 event or command class name, `TenantPurgeWarning` is M27's, `docs/DOMAIN_EVENTS.md`):**

| Key | `eventName` | `recipientType` |
|---|---|---|
| `CHARGE_ISSUED_PERIOD` | `ChargeIssued` | `managerPeriod` |
| `CHARGE_ISSUED_RECOVERY` | `ChargeIssued` | `managerRecovery` |
| `CHARGE_PAID` | `ChargePaid` | `manager` |
| `SUBSCRIPTION_PAST_DUE` | `SubscriptionPastDue` | `manager` |
| `SUBSCRIPTION_SUSPENDED_NON_PAYMENT` | `SubscriptionSuspended` | `managerNonPayment` |
| `SUBSCRIPTION_SUSPENDED_OPERATOR` | `SubscriptionSuspended` | `managerOperator` |
| `SUBSCRIPTION_REACTIVATED` | `SubscriptionReactivated` | `manager` |
| `SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED_IMMEDIATE` | `SubscriptionCancelled` | `managerImmediate` |
| `SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED_SCHEDULED` | `SubscriptionCancelled` | `managerScheduled` |
| `PLAN_CHANGED` | `PlanChanged` | `manager` |
| `BILLING_REMINDER_PLAN_CONFIRMATION` | `BillingReminderDue` | `planConfirmation` |
| `BILLING_REMINDER_TRIAL_ENDING` | `BillingReminderDue` | `trialEnding` |
| `BILLING_REMINDER_CHARGE_DUE_SOON` | `BillingReminderDue` | `chargeDueSoon` |
| `BILLING_REMINDER_CHARGE_OVERDUE` | `BillingReminderDue` | `chargeOverdue` |
| `BILLING_REMINDER_RECOVERY_CHARGE_OPEN` | `BillingReminderDue` | `recoveryChargeOpen` |
| `SUBSCRIPTION_PRICE_CHANGE_ANNOUNCED` | `SubscriptionPriceChangeAnnounced` | `manager` |
| `TENANT_PURGE_WARNING` | `TenantPurgeWarning` | `manager` |

A unit test asserts this table against the mapping (17 rows, each `eventName` an S03 class or `TenantPurgeWarning`), so a renamed S03 event fails S11's spec.

**Backend use case steps:** none (lookup + data).
**Backend HTTP surface:** none.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** one data migration `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/infrastructure/migrations/<timestamp>-SeedBillingTemplates.ts`; the 17 notification entries above in both `notifications.json` files (`eventName.recipientType`, e.g. `ChargeIssued.managerPeriod` / `ChargeIssued.managerRecovery`, `SubscriptionSuspended.managerNonPayment` / `.managerOperator`, `BillingReminderDue.planConfirmation` … `.recoveryChargeOpen`).

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/domain/notification-template-key.enum.ts`, `notification-template-key.mapping.ts` (+ `notification-template-key.mapping.spec.ts`) (modify — the 17 new keys, each entry with `variables` and `platformScoped: true` (the entry type gains `platformScoped`); the `PLATFORM_SCOPED_TEMPLATE_KEYS` constant derived from `platformScoped` and exported from the mapping file; the existing variable/placeholder parity in the mapping spec covers the new copy)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/ports/notification-template-repository.port.ts` (modify — `findPlatformTemplate`; `copyGlobalDefaultsForTenant` excludes the family)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/infrastructure/repositories/typeorm-notification-template.repository.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts`) (modify)
- `apps/backend/src/test/repositories/notification/in-memory-notification-template.repository.ts` (modify — same two behaviours)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/use-cases/seed-default-templates/seed-default-templates.use-case.ts` (+ spec; its only call to `copyGlobalDefaultsForTenant`, asserted to copy none of the family) and `apps/backend/src/shared/database/seed.ts` (modify — its two raw `INSERT … SELECT` copies, `seedNotificationTemplates` and `seedTeamTenant`, exclude the platform-scoped `trigger_event` values; the seed does not call the repository method). **The exclusion is applied in all three SQLs**: the repository's `copyGlobalDefaultsForTenant` (`WHERE tenant_id IS NULL AND locale = $2` in `typeorm-notification-template.repository.ts`) and both raw seed copies (the `INSERT … SELECT` inside `seedNotificationTemplates` and the one inside `seedTeamTenant` in `seed.ts`, which select **all** `tenant_id IS NULL` rows with no locale column); the integration spec covers each of the three
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/infrastructure/migrations/<timestamp>-SeedBillingTemplates.ts` (new)

### M26-S12 — Billing email handlers (UC-124)

**Agent:** `backend-ts + devops`
**Complexity:** L
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-124 and UC-139 (A1: re-check before sending), `docs/BOUNDED_CONTEXTS.md` § Communication Patterns (the event-to-email map), `docs/DOMAIN_EVENTS.md` § Billing Context Events, `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_BACKEND.md` § Event Handlers (Pub/Sub consumers) and § Adding a new notification type, `docs/ANTI_PATTERNS.md` § A domain event is drained, `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_INFRA.md` § Billing configuration (planned), `infra/terraform/README.md` § New-resource PR-sequencing playbook (subscription on an existing topic)
**Dependencies:** M26-S03 (the events and topics), M26-S09 (`BillingReminderDue`), M26-S11 (templates and the lookup), M25-S21a (the mandatory `skipWhenSuspended` declaration on every `subscribe()`), M25-S28 (the `permittedEdges` pattern for a handler that imports a Billing event: Booking's `fan-out-tenant-unavailable-notice` is the reference entry; this story adds its own Notification → Billing entries)
**Pattern:** One event handler per event, each calling exactly one send use case (`BaseNotificationUseCase` Template Method); idempotent on `eventId`; global-template lookup (S11).

**Description:**
Ikaro sends every Billing email itself (Asaas customers are created with `notificationDisabled = true`). For each of `ChargeIssued` (payment link), `ChargePaid` (receipt link), `SubscriptionPastDue`, `SubscriptionSuspended`, `SubscriptionReactivated`, `SubscriptionCancelled`, `PlanChanged` (when `effectiveFrom` is `null`, a selection saved while suspended, the copy says the plan takes effect when the reactivation payment is confirmed, never a date; UC-136 A14), `BillingReminderDue` (all five kinds) and `SubscriptionPriceChangeAnnounced` (one consolidated email per tenant: "o valor de X passa de R$ 39 para R$ 49 a partir de 10/12; seu novo total mensal será R$ …"), a handler class (names unique across the codebase) subscribes with its own consumer name and calls one send use case, passing `event.correlationId`. **Every one of the nine `subscribe()` calls declares `{ skipWhenSuspended: false }`** (M25-S21a\'s explicit-declaration detector fails CI without a declaration; `true` would make the dispatcher skip `SubscriptionSuspended` and the recovery `ChargeIssued` for exactly the suspended tenant and defeat the acceptance criterion below). **Data source — events only:** the recipients, the payment link and the receipt link are in the event data (`ChargeIssued.paymentUrl`, `ChargePaid` receipt URL — added in S03); the recipient is the event's nullable `recipientEmail` (resolved by Billing, S03 — the single documented exception to "no email in payload"); when it is null Notification falls back to the tenant's MANAGER emails through the existing `INotificationStaffPort.getManagerEmails`, and reads locale and tenant details through the existing `INotificationPlatformPort` — **no Billing read port and no new cross-context edge in the Notification → Billing direction beyond the event classes**, each of which needs an exact-path `permittedEdges` entry. **Recipients:** the event's `recipientEmail` if non-null, else the tenant's managers (`INotificationStaffPort.getManagerEmails`). **Freshness and exempt tenants are Billing's responsibility, not Notification's (R3):** Pub/Sub is at-least-once with no delivery-time guarantee and Notification has no Billing read port, so **Notification does no re-check and no exempt check**. Billing re-checks "charge still open" and "subscription not exempt" immediately before writing the outbox row of any email-triggering event or command, never publishes an email-triggering event for an `exempt` subscription, and events raised from exempt paths (for example the set-exempt hook's `SubscriptionReactivated`) are not emailed: Billing does not publish the email-triggering event at all for those paths (the use case that sets the exempt flag decides this before the outbox write; S20 fills that hook). A small stale window is accepted; reminder and `ChargeIssued` copy is tolerant ("Se você já pagou, desconsidere este aviso"). Redelivery is deduplicated on `eventId` via the notification log (A3); a delivery failure uses the existing failed / retry handling (A2); a missing platform template alerts (A1). Billing emails use the platform lookup (S11) and their handlers are exempt from the suspension gate (`skipWhenSuspended: false`, declared below). **Consumer names and the suspended pair.** `SubscriptionSuspended` already has M25-S28's customer-notice consumer, a Booking handler (`fan-out-tenant-unavailable-notice`) that raises `TenantUnavailableNoticeDue` for Notification's `notify-customer-tenant-unavailable` (the reason-neutral email to customers); `SubscriptionReactivated` has no S28 consumer. **`SubscriptionSuspended` therefore already has three consumers** (`audit-log`, Booking's `fan-out-tenant-unavailable-notice` and Platform's `revalidate-hotsite-on-suspension`, `pubsub-catalog.json`) and `SubscriptionReactivated` two (`audit-log`, `revalidate-hotsite-on-reactivation`); this story adds a fourth and a third. This story's manager handlers use their own distinct consumer names (`send-subscription-suspended-notification`, `send-subscription-reactivated-notification`) and their own push subscriptions — independent consumers of the same topic, never merged. **File name vs consumer name:** the handler file is `<event-kebab>-notification.handler.ts` and the consumer name (a `static readonly CONSUMER_NAME` on the class, the key the catalog and `modules/pubsub` use) is `send-<event-kebab>-notification` for all nine, so one rule maps a file to its subscription. **One template key per wording (S11).** The handler/use case picks the key from event data: `ChargeIssued.kind` → `CHARGE_ISSUED_PERIOD` or `CHARGE_ISSUED_RECOVERY`; `SubscriptionCancelled.immediate` → `SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED_IMMEDIATE` or `_SCHEDULED`; `BillingReminderDue.kind` → the matching `BILLING_REMINDER_*` key; `SubscriptionSuspended.reason` as below. **What the manager email does by suspension reason** (read from the event's reason; product default, DEFERRABLE): `NON_PAYMENT` / `PAYMENT_REVERSED` → `SUBSCRIPTION_SUSPENDED_NON_PAYMENT`: "your account is suspended, pay one month to reactivate", linking the **billing page** (the dashboard path `/dashboard/billing`, reachable while suspended) — **not** a payment link, because the recovery charge is created at the provider after commit and the suspension event cannot carry its URL; the email says the payment link follows in the next email (the recovery `ChargeIssued`, `CHARGE_ISSUED_RECOVERY`, from S06), and points to "Dados de cobrança" when the billing address is missing (a held recovery charge has no `ChargeIssued`, R1); `CANCELED` → **no manager email from this handler** (the manager gets exactly one email per cancel: `SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED_*`; the same for a UC-142 deactivation); `OPERATOR` → `SUBSCRIPTION_SUSPENDED_OPERATOR`: the manager is still told, with **no payment link and no reactivation instructions** (only the operator can lift it; there is no recovery charge), saying to contact Ikaro. The purge-warning email (`TenantPurgeWarning`) is **not** wired here — M27 owns its handler (its template ships in S11).

**Devops half (nine new subscriptions: two on topics that already exist — `SubscriptionSuspended` and `SubscriptionReactivated` (M25-S15b) —, six on the topics S03 created (`ChargeIssued`, `ChargePaid`, `SubscriptionPastDue`, `SubscriptionCancelled`, `PlanChanged`, `SubscriptionPriceChangeAnnounced`) and one on a topic that does not exist yet: `BillingReminderDue`'s, which this story\'s `subscribe()` call site creates).** **PR sequence:** 1 PR + 1 Foundation apply per the playbook row "A new Pub/Sub topic (… cron-published `Command` with a consumer …) + its app code" (the README has no separate row for a subscription on an existing topic: row 2 covers the `subscribe()` call site and its subscription) — PR1 (`infra-app-mix-ok`): the handlers' `subscribe()` call sites, `pubsub-catalog.json` regenerated (subscriptions and their DLQs are derived from the catalog by `modules/pubsub`; no per-subscription `envs/*` edit, as M25-S11 records); then the manual Foundation apply. **Staging un-pause (S09):** the same PR removes `cron-billing-reminders` from `paused_job_keys` in `infra/terraform/envs/staging/main.tf` (S09 added it because `BillingReminderDue` had no topic); that env-root edit ships through the normal staging deploy, not the Foundation apply, and **production stays paused** (S39 enables it). **IAM / permissions:** push-subscription invoker per subscription, derived from the catalog; no new principal. **Live-verification check:** on staging after the apply: `gcloud pubsub subscriptions describe <each>` shows the push endpoint; then provision a throwaway staging tenant with a manager email you own, advance time and issue a charge (precondition: S13's `simulate` / `seed` endpoints are merged — S12 does not depend on S13; otherwise use M25-S12's clock endpoint plus a manual `gcloud scheduler jobs run` of the issue job, or a one-off backend REPL call publishing the event), and confirm one `ChargeIssued` email arrives (Mailhog is local only; on staging the tenant manager's own inbox) and the notification log row is `SENT`. Pass = subscriptions described + one email delivered.

**Backend use case steps:**
1. One `Send<Event>NotificationUseCase` per event (nine, thin, over `BaseNotificationUseCase`), 2. one handler class per event under `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/infrastructure/events/` — the Notification context owns sending, so the handlers live in **Notification** (consumers of Billing events, listed in `docs/BOUNDED_CONTEXTS.md` § Communication Patterns), never under `contexts/billing`, subscribing with consumer names `send-<event-kebab>-notification`.
**Backend HTTP surface:** none — event-driven.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** none.

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/use-cases/send-charge-issued-notification/send-charge-issued-notification.use-case.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/use-cases/send-charge-paid-notification/send-charge-paid-notification.use-case.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/use-cases/send-subscription-past-due-notification/send-subscription-past-due-notification.use-case.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/use-cases/send-subscription-suspended-notification/send-subscription-suspended-notification.use-case.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/use-cases/send-subscription-reactivated-notification/send-subscription-reactivated-notification.use-case.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/use-cases/send-subscription-cancelled-notification/send-subscription-cancelled-notification.use-case.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/use-cases/send-plan-changed-notification/send-plan-changed-notification.use-case.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/use-cases/send-billing-reminder-notification/send-billing-reminder-notification.use-case.ts`, `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/application/use-cases/send-price-change-announced-notification/send-price-change-announced-notification.use-case.ts` (+ `.use-case.spec.ts` and `.use-case.integration.spec.ts` beside each) (new — nine folders following the existing `send-booking-*` sibling folders)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/notification/infrastructure/events/charge-issued-notification.handler.ts`, `charge-paid-notification.handler.ts`, `subscription-past-due-notification.handler.ts`, `subscription-suspended-notification.handler.ts`, `subscription-reactivated-notification.handler.ts`, `subscription-cancelled-notification.handler.ts`, `plan-changed-notification.handler.ts`, `billing-reminder-notification.handler.ts`, `price-change-announced-notification.handler.ts` (+ `.spec.ts` each) (new — class names are the PascalCase of the file names, unique across the codebase)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] The manager (or the billing email) receives the right email, in their language, for each billing event, once; exempt tenants receive none (Billing never publishes for them, R3).
- [ ] A manager gets exactly one email for a cancel (the `SUBSCRIPTION_CANCELLED_*` one; none for the `CANCELED` suspension).
- [ ] A reminder or invoice email whose charge was paid in the meantime carries tolerant wording ("Se você já pagou, desconsidere este aviso"); the freshness check itself is Billing's, at publish time (S09 / S05).

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit:
  - [ ] Each use case: recipient resolution (event `recipientEmail` vs the managers fallback; the `OPERATOR` key without a payment link), template-key selection from event data (`ChargeIssued.kind`, `SubscriptionCancelled.immediate`, `SubscriptionSuspended.reason`, `BillingReminderDue.kind`), `reason = CANCELED` sends nothing, locale, variables per template; no Billing read and no re-check in Notification (the use case has no Billing port)
  - [ ] Each handler calls exactly one use case, rethrows, passes `correlationId`, and its `subscribe()` declares `skipWhenSuspended: false` (the M25-S21a detector passes)
  - [ ] **Negative guarantee (decided in M26-S03 review):** a `SubscriptionReactivated` whose `exempt` is `true` sends no email and writes no log row, even though its `recipientEmail` is `null` (which alone means "no billing email set" and falls back to the managers); pinned by a test through the real handler
- Integration:
  - [ ] End to end through the in-memory bus on the fake: each event → one log row `SENT`; redelivery of the same `eventId` creates no second row
  - [ ] Missing platform template → failure plus a structured error log with the fixed field `event: "billing_template_missing"` (the log-based metric S36's alert reads), not a silent drop
  - [ ] A suspended tenant still receives its `SubscriptionSuspended` / `ChargeIssued (recovery)` email (the handler's `skipWhenSuspended: false`); an `OPERATOR` suspension sends the manager email without a payment link, and M25-S28's customer notice is unaffected and independent
- Tenant isolation: [ ] emails go only to the event's tenant's recipients; the log row carries that `tenantId`
- E2E: none — covered by integration; live check above
- [ ] Checkov clean on the one `.tf` edit (the staging `paused_job_keys` change; Tfsec is in no CI gate)
- [ ] After the staging deploy the `ikaro-cron-billing-reminders` Scheduler job is enabled in staging and still paused in production (`gcloud scheduler jobs describe`)

### M26-S13 — Dev `simulate` and `seed` endpoints (UC-138)

**Agent:** `backend-ts`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-138 (all of it), `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Provider Webhooks and Internal API (non-production endpoints), `docs/TESTING_STRATEGY.md` § Testing the billing provider — fake, sandbox, contract suite and clock, `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_INFRA.md` § Billing configuration (planned), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_TESTING.md` § Billing provider tests (planned)
**Dependencies:** M26-S04 (the backend internal webhook route the fake delivers to), M26-S05 (charge issuing), M26-S06 (statuses), M25-S12 (clock, the dev controller pattern and the `APP_ENV` rule), M25-S14 (the fake's delivery options and `BillingProviderRegistry`), M25-S15a (`PlatformAdminGuard` in shared), M25-S09 / M25-S10 (the registrations and `POST /internal/tenants` routes the seed script calls; `exempt` is a field of the OPERATOR registration), M25-S15b (`SetTenantExemptUseCase`), M25-S16 / M25-S20 (gating decorators on the dev routes)
**Pattern:** Test-support endpoints registered only in `local` / `staging`; the normal path processes every simulated event (**no test-only path inside any use case**).

**Description:**
Let a developer or operator drive every billing state without a provider account or waiting. `POST /internal/dev/billing/provider/simulate` `{ tenantId | chargeId, event, delivery, failure? }` — `event ∈ PAID_PIX | PAID_BOLETO | PAID_CARD | OVERDUE | REFUND_FULL | REFUND_PARTIAL | CHARGEBACK_OPENED | CHARGEBACK_LOST`; `delivery ∈ NORMAL | DUPLICATE | DELAYED | OUT_OF_ORDER`; `failure ∈ PROVIDER_5XX | LINK_EXPIRED | INVALID_LOCATION` (fails the next provider call). The `FakeBillingProvider` builds the webhook payload (with the verified per-method sequences) and **delivers it in-process / by loopback to the backend internal webhook route `POST /internal/billing/webhooks/{provider}` (S04, `InternalApiGuard`)** — not to the BFF public route: the static `asaas-access-token` is verified only by the BFF (a BFF-only secret, S01/S04), so the BFF token check is covered by S04's own BFF tests and `simulate` exercises everything behind it (normalisation, inbox, `ApplyChargeFactUseCase`); the normal path processes it, no test-only path exists in any use case. **Which charge:** with `chargeId` that charge; with `tenantId` the tenant's single open charge for the `PAID_*` / `OVERDUE` events and its most recent `PAID` charge for `REFUND_*` / `CHARGEBACK_*`; exactly one of `tenantId` / `chargeId` is required (`400` otherwise). **When the event does not apply** (a paid event for a charge that is not open, a refund for a charge that is not paid, no matching charge) the endpoint returns `409` with a Problem Details body and delivers nothing. `DELAYED` and `OUT_OF_ORDER` deliveries are produced by the fake as a sequence delivered within the request (the delayed one after a short in-request wait, the out-of-order pair reversed), never by a background timer. Works **only** for a subscription whose `merchant_profile_key` is a `*_FAKE` key (A2: sandbox / live keys → refused; staging sandboxes are driven by the provider's own sandbox). **Seed is a script, not a Billing-owned provisioning path.** Creating a tenant needs Platform provisioning, the signup registration and Staff, and M26 adds no Billing → Platform provisioning edge (milestone "Cross-context edges"). So `scripts/billing-seed.sh` (the `scripts/provision-tenant.sh` precedent) **orchestrates HTTP**: `POST /internal/billing/registrations` (with `exempt: true` for the exempt state), `POST /internal/tenants` (with the returned `registrationId`), `POST /internal/dev/clock/advance`, and `POST /internal/dev/billing/provider/simulate` plus the cron publishers (`POST /cron/billing-issue-charges`, `…/billing-status-sweep` — the manual route keeps the short name, the trigger constant is `cron-billing-*`) to reach each state — one tenant per state with **fixed slugs** (`billing-seed-trialing`, `-active`, `-past-due`, `-suspended`, `-exempt`), so a re-run finds the existing tenants and only advances what is missing (re-runnable). The `exempt` state may also be reached through `SetTenantExemptUseCase` (M25-S15b). The backend endpoint `POST /internal/dev/billing/seed` only drives the **Billing-side** steps of that sequence (`SeedBillingStatesUseCase`: given an existing seeded tenant id and a target state, advance charges and statuses through the normal use cases); it creates no tenant. States: `TRIALING`, `ACTIVE`, `PAST_DUE`, `SUSPENDED` for `NON_PAYMENT`, `exempt`. **The US variants (US tenant with and without address, UC-138) are M28** and are not seeded here; record that in the milestone Coverage / Non-Goals. The two states that need a cancel — `ACTIVE` with a scheduled cancel and `SUSPENDED` for `CANCELED` — are added by **S20**, which extends `SeedBillingStatesUseCase` and the script (S13 cannot reach them without S20's cancel use case and deliberately takes no S20 dependency). Both endpoints sit behind `InternalApiGuard` + `PlatformAdminGuard`, are registered **only when `APP_ENV ∈ {local, staging}`** and do not exist (404) in production (A1); missing keys → `401` (A3). **Production safety:** `APP_ENV` defaults to `local` in `config/env.validation.ts`, so registration also requires an explicit positive signal and a **startup test refuses `NODE_ENV=production` with `APP_ENV` unset or `local`** (the whole check: a live `merchant_profile_key` is a database column that startup cannot see, so that rule stays where it already is, `BillingProviderRegistry.resolve()` / `assertMerchantProfileKeyAllowed`, UC-138 A1). It is **not** `NODE_ENV=production` ⇒ `APP_ENV=production`: the staging Cloud Run services set `NODE_ENV = "production"` with `APP_ENV = "staging"` (`infra/terraform/envs/staging/main.tf`), so the rule only forbids the `local` value (explicit or defaulted) under `NODE_ENV=production`. The check lives in `env.validation.ts` (a refinement with its spec) and therefore **also covers the existing M25-S12 `DevClockModule`**, which is gated by `APP_ENV` alone today; the production Terraform sets `APP_ENV = "production"` (`infra/terraform/envs/prod/main.tf`). **Decided at the M26 audit (2026-11-18): staging test subscriptions carry `BRL_FAKE` until S37**, so `simulate` works on staging for them (a `*_FAKE` key is allowed in staging by `assertMerchantProfileKeyAllowed`); the provisioning path resolves `BRL_ASAAS_SANDBOX` there, so `SeedBillingStatesUseCase` sets the seeded subscription's `merchant_profile_key` to `BRL_FAKE` on `staging` before any charge exists and refuses to change the key of a subscription that already has a charge (the exact write path is confirmed at `/story-discovery`). Why: the registry has no adapter for the sandbox key until S37, so without this staging could not drive any state. `seed` runs with the single-instance dev clock (M25-S12 rule). Scripts `scripts/billing-simulate.sh` and `scripts/billing-seed.sh` wrap them.

**Backend use case steps:**
1. **`SimulateProviderEventUseCase`** (fake only), 2. **`SeedBillingStatesUseCase`**.
**Backend HTTP surface:** new `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/internal-dev-billing.controller.ts` — `POST /internal/dev/billing/provider/simulate`, `POST /internal/dev/billing/seed` (`InternalApiGuard` + `PlatformAdminGuard`, `@NoFeatureGate()` and `@SuspensionAccess('ANY_AUTHENTICATED')` exactly as `internal-dev-clock.controller.ts`; registered through a `DevBillingModule` that mirrors `DevClockModule`: production gets an empty module, `local` and `staging` get the controller and the two use cases).
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** none.

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/use-cases/simulate-provider-event.use-case.ts`, `seed-billing-states.use-case.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts`) (new)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/internal-dev-billing.controller.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts`) (new)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/dev-billing.module.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`) (new — `DevBillingModule.register(appEnv)` mirroring `dev-clock.module.ts` and its spec: empty in `production`, controller + use cases otherwise), `apps/backend/src/app.module.ts` (modify — import `DevBillingModule.register(resolveAppEnv(process.env))` beside `DevClockModule`)
- `apps/backend/src/config/env.validation.ts` (+ `env.validation.spec.ts`) (modify — the startup refusal of `NODE_ENV=production` with `APP_ENV` `local`/unset)
- `apps/backend/http/billing/dev-billing.http` (new)

- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/providers/fake-billing.provider.ts` (modify — M25-S14 left it queueing: the `simulate*` methods enqueue and `drainDeliveries(options)` hands the payloads out, with no delivery; add the in-request delivery of each drained payload to the backend internal webhook route (the loopback described above), keeping `DUPLICATE` / `DELAYED` / `OUT_OF_ORDER` in the existing `DeliveryOptions`)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A developer can put a local tenant into each billing state (the two cancel states once S20 lands) and fire each provider event (including duplicated and out-of-order) in one command; production cannot.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit:
  - [ ] Controllers are registered only when `APP_ENV` is `local` or `staging` and not registered for any other value (production included); `NODE_ENV=production` with `APP_ENV` unset or `local` fails startup (and a staging-shaped `NODE_ENV=production` + `APP_ENV=staging` boots), covering the existing clock module too; `simulate` refused for a non-`*_FAKE` key
  - [ ] `simulate` input: exactly one of `tenantId` / `chargeId` (`400` otherwise); charge selection by event kind with `tenantId`; a non-applicable event → `409` and nothing delivered
- Integration:
  - [ ] Each `event` × `delivery` combination produces exactly the state a real event would (reusing the S04 assertions through the HTTP path)
  - [ ] The seed script, run twice, leaves one tenant per documented state under the fixed slugs (the two cancel states arrive with S20) and creates them only through normal use cases / routes; the Billing-side endpoint creates no tenant
  - [ ] Missing / wrong operator keys → `401`; production-configured app context → `404`
  - [ ] On `APP_ENV=staging` the seeded subscription is on `BRL_FAKE` and `simulate` works on it; a subscription that already has a charge is never re-keyed (D1, staging until S37)
  - [ ] **Live check carried from S03:** on a `BRL_FAKE` staging subscription, one event published through `simulate` (e.g. `ChargePaid`) is logged by the `audit-log` consumer (confirms the six S03 topics and subscriptions deliver end to end)
- Tenant isolation: [ ] `simulate` targets one tenant's subscription/charge; a charge id of another tenant is not found
- E2E: none — covered by integration (S35 uses these endpoints)
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

## Wave 2 — Manager backend / BFF

> The manager endpoints share one BFF controller (`apps/bff/src/features/billing/billing.controller.ts`, created in M25-S13 for `GET /v1/billing/access`) with sibling `billing.schemas.ts` / `billing.types.ts` / `billing.mapper.ts`, and one backend controller family under `contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/`. Every BFF route is `@Roles('MANAGER')` and **carries no tenant-access annotation: the BFF has no gate (M25-S33), it forwards the backend's typed denial unchanged**. **The backend `billing.controller.ts` carries `@NoFeatureGate()` (no sellable feature owns the billing surface) + `@SuspensionAccess(MANAGER_BILLING)` (M25-S16) on every route, so a suspended manager can reach it** (the M25-S16 global `TenantAccessGuard` runs in the backend and defaults an unannotated route to suspension-denied; the `tenant-access-route-metadata` detector is enforcing after M25-S24) and reads the actor role from `X-Actor-Role`; each story below therefore lists the decorator specs in its AC. Tenant scope comes from the JWT and `RequestContext`, never a request field. **Error-code ownership (M26 rule):** the story that first throws a `BILLING_*` code owns its `packages/types/src/error-codes.ts` member, both `packages/i18n/locales/{pt-BR,en}/errors.json` entries, the error mapper and, for BFF-thrown codes, `BffThrowableCode` (`apps/bff/src/shared/http/problem-detail.ts`); M25-S06 translated only `SIGNUP_TAX_ID_REQUIRED`, `SIGNUP_TAX_ID_INVALID` and `BILLING_IDENTITY_TAX_ID_INVALID`. Because they edit the same BFF files, **the Wave 2 BFF stories are sequential in practice** (see `## Run-batch preview`).

### M26-S14 — Manager reads — summary and charge history (UC-117), plus the operator list's Charge-derived fields (UC-111)

**Agent:** `backend-ts + bff-ts`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-117 (all), UC-130 (banner states), `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Manager API (BFF, role MANAGER) (summary response, charge history item, placement and gating), `docs/DOMAIN_MODEL.md` § Context 7: Billing Context (`suspendsAt`, `purgeAt`, `paymentConfirmationPending`), `docs/BFF_ARCHITECTURE.md` § Module & Controller Naming Conventions, `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Base Standards (Pattern A pagination, money)
**Dependencies:** M26-S02 (charge repository, listing), M25-S13 (BFF billing module), M25-S16 (`@SuspensionAccess`)
**Pattern:** Query use cases (read models) + BFF mapper shaping the response (enums, no display strings); Pattern A pagination.

**Description:**
The read side of the billing page. `GET /v1/billing/summary` returns the documented shape: `status`, `exempt`, `suspensionReason` (only while `SUSPENDED`), `currency`/`countryCode`, `timezone` (the subscription's immutable IANA zone, so the web formats instants in it, never in the browser zone), `cycle` (always `MONTHLY` in M26) with `nextCycle` / `nextCycleFrom` (always `null` in M26 — owned by M30, present so the contract does not change), `trialEndsAt`, `nextDueDate`, `pastDueSince`, `suspendsAt` (= `coalesce(grace_until, past_due_since + 14 days)`, `PAST_DUE` only), `cancelAt`, `purgeAt` (only `SUSPENDED`; `suspended_at` + 90 days when `has_ever_paid` is false, + 180 days when true, `docs/DOMAIN_MODEL.md` § Context 7 ("computed, not stored independently") — returned as the **earliest** clean-up date, never a promise: no job acts on it in M26, so the web (S27) labels it "a partir de" / "earliest", not as a scheduled deletion), `planConfirmationRequired` / `planConfirmationFallbackAt` (`TRIALING` with `plan_confirmed_at` null; fallback = `trial_ends_at − 3 days`), `canDiscard` (**always `false` in M26** — M27), `firstChargePaid` (**added here, Decided at the M26 audit (2026-11-18)**: true when the subscription's first `PERIOD` charge is `PAID`; the cancel page (S31, UC-133 A3) cannot otherwise tell whether a trial that cancels has paid anything, and its wording must say so; one `EXISTS` over the charge repository, `tenant_id`-scoped), `addressRequired` (the full billing address is not saved), `items[]` (feature, tier, `unitPriceMinor` for display — items store no price, so each is read from a price version: for `ACTIVE` / `PAST_DUE` / `SUSPENDED` the items running now at the version in force today; **for `TRIALING` the plan that will be invoiced first** — the items scheduled for the first paid period (the default paid items starting at `trialEndsAt`, or the manager's confirmed selection), at the version in force on that period's start date, which is what S05 will charge; a trial's items are never "active now", so reading `startsAt <= now` would return an empty list), `totalMinor` (net of tax; BR has none), `openCharge` (the open `PERIOD` charge, or while `SUSPENDED` the open `RECOVERY` charge, with `hasPaymentLink` / `paymentLinkExpiresAt`; never a URL), `autoBilling` (`NONE`/`INACTIVE`, label null — the "Em breve" tab), `paymentConfirmationPending` (true while `payment_return_seen_at` is set and the charge is still open), and `upcomingPriceChanges[]` (dated list-price changes affecting the tenant's items whose version has `effective_from` in the future, `announced_at IS NOT NULL` and `superseded_at IS NULL` — a version the operator created but whose fan-out has not run, or a superseded one, is never shown). `GET /v1/billing/charges?limit=&offset=` returns the paginated history (Pattern A, `(tenant_id, due_date DESC)`): `id, kind, periodStart|null, periodEnd|null, dueDate, subtotalMinor, taxMinor (0), amountMinor, currency, status, method, paidAt, receiptUrl|null, lines[], taxLines ([])`. **A tenant with no subscription row (UC-117 A9, UC-120 A1: the provisioning window, or a tenant self-heal has not repaired yet) — OPEN: decide at /story-discovery** between (a) `200` with `provisioning: true` and every other field `null`/empty (recommended: no new error code, the web renders "preparando sua conta" with a retry, S27) and (b) a typed `404` with a new `BILLING_SUBSCRIPTION_NOT_FOUND` code (needs the code, both locale entries and an `ERROR_CATALOG` row); either way the web never shows a generic error for it. The summary is never cached (UC-117; the BFF has no access cache since M25-S33) and an `exempt` tenant gets `exempt: true` with no actions. A staff or customer token → `403`. The BFF mapper returns **enums and flags, not display strings** (the BFF has no i18n: `apps/bff/src` imports nothing from `@ikaro/i18n`, and CLAUDE.md §7 puts visible copy in the web's `pt-BR` / `en` locale files); the web (S27) translates the status labels and the "Conta isenta de cobrança" notice from `status` / `exempt`. The mapper only shapes the response (flattens `openCharge`, drops internal fields, never emits a URL).

**Operator list extension (UC-111; owner of the work M25 promised).** M25-S15b's read-only `GET /internal/billing/tenants?status=` (`internal-billing-operator.controller.ts`) returns only subscription-derived fields in M25; this story, which already owns the Charge repository reads, adds its Charge-derived fields: **overdue age** (days since the oldest `OVERDUE` open charge's `due_date`, in the subscription's timezone), **last charge** (latest non-`CANCELED` charge: kind, status, amount, due date) and **open recovery** (the open `RECOVERY` charge, if any). The extension reads charges through the S02 repository in one batched, `tenant_id`-scoped query per page (no per-row query, no N+1), keeps the list offset-paginated and an investigation lookup (never a worklist), and changes no operator write.

**Backend use case steps:**
1. **`GetBillingSummaryUseCase`**, 2. **`ListBillingChargesUseCase`**, 3. **the existing `ListTenantBillingStatusUseCase`** (`list-tenant-billing-status.use-case.ts`, M25-S15b; modified to add the Charge-derived fields above).
**Backend HTTP surface:** new controller `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/billing.controller.ts` — `GET /billing/summary`, `GET /billing/charges` (tenant from `RequestContext`; exact prefix mirrors the nearest sibling, e.g. `TenantSettingsController`, confirmed at story discovery).
**BFF endpoint spec:** extends `apps/bff/src/features/billing/billing.controller.ts` — `GET /v1/billing/summary`, `GET /v1/billing/charges?limit=&offset=` (`@Roles('MANAGER')`); response types in `packages/types/src/billing.dto.ts`. The backend routes carry `@NoFeatureGate()` + `@SuspensionAccess(MANAGER_BILLING)`; the BFF routes carry no annotation (Wave 2 note, M25-S33).
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** none (no new error code is thrown here; labels are web locale keys added by S27).

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/use-cases/get-billing-summary.use-case.ts`, `list-billing-charges.use-case.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts`) (new)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/billing.controller.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts`) (new)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/internal-billing-operator.controller.ts` (+ spec, integration spec) and `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/use-cases/list-tenant-billing-status.use-case.ts` (+ spec) (modify — Charge-derived fields)
- `apps/backend/http/billing/billing.http` (new)
- `apps/bff/src/features/billing/billing.controller.ts`, `billing.schemas.ts`, `billing.types.ts`, `billing.mapper.ts` (+ specs) (modify)
- `apps/bff/src/features/billing/billing.controller.component.spec.ts` (modify — created in M25-S13)
- `apps/bff/http/billing/billing.http` (new)
- `packages/types/src/billing.dto.ts` (modify), `packages/types/src/index.ts` (modify only if a new export is needed)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/billing.module.ts` (modify)
- `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` (modify — § Billing — Manager API: add `firstChargePaid` (and, if option (a) is chosen, `provisioning`) to the summary shape and field notes; the operator-list row already names the Charge-derived fields)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A manager sees the status, dates, active features with prices, total, open charge, announced price changes and the full history; a trial, active, past-due, suspended (with the recovery charge and clean-up date), exempt and cancelling tenant each render correctly.
- [ ] A staff member or a customer cannot read any of it.
- [ ] A tenant still inside the provisioning window (no subscription row) gets the defined "preparando sua conta" answer, not an error (UC-117 A9).

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit:
  - [ ] A tenant without a subscription row returns the answer chosen at discovery (no throw, no 500)
  - [ ] `firstChargePaid`: false for a trial with no charge or a `PENDING` first charge, true once the first `PERIOD` charge is `PAID`, and it stays true after a later cancel
  - [ ] Summary per status (table-driven): `suspendsAt` with and without `grace_until`, `purgeAt` = `suspended_at` + 90 days for never-paid vs + 180 days for `has_ever_paid`, `planConfirmationRequired`/fallback date, `canDiscard` false, `paymentConfirmationPending`
  - [ ] An item price read from the version in force today (a future version is only in `upcomingPriceChanges`)
  - [ ] Mapper: `exempt` flag, enum status, never a URL in `openCharge`; `timezone`, `nextCycle` / `nextCycleFrom` (null) present
  - [ ] A `TRIALING` summary lists the first-paid-period items priced at the version in force on that period's start; `upcomingPriceChanges` excludes a version with `announced_at` null or `superseded_at` set
- Integration:
  - [ ] The backend routes carry `@NoFeatureGate()` + `@SuspensionAccess(MANAGER_BILLING)` (decorator spec; a suspended manager reaches them); the BFF routes carry no annotation (M25-S33)
  - [ ] Pagination order/limit/offset and `total`/`hasMore`; the query uses the `(tenant_id, due_date DESC)` index (`EXPLAIN`)
  - [ ] Summary of a suspended tenant returns the open `RECOVERY` charge, not the stale period charge
  - [ ] `STAFF` and `CUSTOMER` tokens → `403`; reachable for a `SUSPENDED` tenant's manager
- Tenant isolation: [ ] tenant A's manager never receives tenant B's charges or subscription (explicit cross-tenant integration test, for `GET /billing/summary` and for `GET /billing/charges` with both tenants holding charges, and for the operator list's batched charge query, which must not leak one tenant's last charge into another's row)
- Operator list (UC-111): [ ] `GET /internal/billing/tenants?status=` returns overdue age, last charge and open recovery for a `PAST_DUE` and a `SUSPENDED` tenant, and `null`s for a tenant with no charge; one batched charge query per page (no N+1); `InternalApiGuard` + `PlatformAdminGuard` unchanged
- E2E: none — covered by integration; the page E2E is S35
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S15 — Payment link and payment return (UC-119)

**Agent:** `backend-ts + bff-ts`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-119 (all, A1–A5), UC-116 (the on-demand refresh), UC-117 A3, `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Manager API (the two charge routes), `docs/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md` § `billing.charges` (`payment_return_seen_at`), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_BACKEND.md` § Billing (partly built) and § Transactions
**Dependencies:** M26-S02 (charge), M26-S07 (`RefreshChargeFromProviderUseCase`), M26-S14 (BFF controller and types)
**Pattern:** Command use cases; provider I/O **outside** the transaction (M26-D3); reuse of the shared refresh (M26-D1).

**Description:**
Let the manager pay. `POST /v1/billing/charges/{chargeId}/payment-link` → `200 { paymentUrl, expiresAt }`: loads the tenant's charge; if it is not open (`PENDING`, `OVERDUE`, or the `RECOVERY` charge while suspended) → `BILLING_CHARGE_NOT_PAYABLE` (default recommendation `409`; **Decided at the M26 audit (2026-11-18): not payable = `CANCELED`, `REFUNDED`, `CHARGEBACK`** — `FAILED` is reserved for M29 and never occurs in M26, and `docs/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md` already says a fresh link on a failed charge starts a new provider attempt, so when M29 introduces `FAILED` it is payable; `PAID` is not an error (A4); the final HTTP status is confirmed at story discovery); an already-paid charge returns the paid state so the page refreshes (A4). When the stored link is expired or missing the use case asks `BillingProviderPort.regeneratePaymentLink` (a port method already built by M25-S14 and listed in `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Provider port, M26-D10 — no port change here) (A1: Asaas's `invoiceUrl` has no documented expiry — the adapter re-reads the charge and returns the current URL; behaviour after the due date is re-verified by S37). **`expiresAt` (H8):** `ProviderPaymentLink.expiresAt` is non-null in the port while `charges.payment_url_expires_at` is nullable; S37 defines the Asaas adapter's value (for example the due date plus a fixed number of days) and the fake returns a real one, so this story stores whatever the port returns and the response's `expiresAt` is never invented here. **Locking (no lost update):** `charges` has no `version` column (`docs/DATABASE_SCHEMA.md` § `billing.charges`), so the use case reads the charge, calls the provider with no lock held (M26-D3), then opens a short transaction that takes the subscription row lock (`findByTenantIdForUpdate`, B-R1, M26-D2), **reloads the charge**, and writes only the link fields (`payment_url`, `payment_url_expires_at`) — a webhook that marked the charge `PAID` in between is never overwritten (the reloaded status wins; the stored link is still kept). A charge that has `provider_creation_status = PENDING` and no provider id (a provider outage, not an address hold) has no link to regenerate: the route answers `BILLING_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE` (default `503`, retryable; S05's retry creates it) without a provider call. `POST /v1/billing/charges/{chargeId}/payment-return` is called by the web when the manager returns from the hosted page (the `returnUrl` that brings the manager back, its `?payment=return&charge={id}` marker and the web-origin env var are owned by S05, which passes `returnUrl` to `createCharge`; this story and S28 only consume the marker): it records `payment_return_seen_at` (which lengthens the S06 settlement margin for a boleto that has not compensated) — written under the same B-R1 lock + charge reload as above, set once, idempotent — and calls `RefreshChargeFromProviderUseCase` (S07) for an on-demand single-charge refresh; the response is the current charge state `{ status, paymentConfirmationPending }` and **never fails** because the provider is unreachable (the page keeps polling, A2). A `RECOVERY` charge created by the status sweep and still held for a missing billing identity or address (the one hold on a recovery charge — S06 / S05; a manager's own reactivate never creates one, S20) has no link to give: the route answers `BILLING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED` (default `422`; **this story is its first thrower and owns the code**, reused by S19 and S20) without a provider call and the page sends the manager to "Dados de cobrança". A payment that fails leaves the charge open and the page offers "Tentar pagar novamente" (A3 — no server state).

**Backend use case steps:**
1. **`GetOrRegenerateChargePaymentLinkUseCase`**, 2. **`RecordPaymentReturnUseCase`** (record, then refresh).
**Backend HTTP surface:** extends `billing.controller.ts` — `POST /billing/charges/:chargeId/payment-link`, `POST /billing/charges/:chargeId/payment-return`.
**BFF endpoint spec:** extends the BFF billing controller — `POST /v1/billing/charges/{chargeId}/payment-link` and `…/payment-return` (`@Roles('MANAGER')`; `chargeId` through `CanonicalParseUUIDPipe`); request: none; responses as above. The backend routes carry the same decorators.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** error codes **new** (none exists in `packages/types/src/error-codes.ts` today; first thrown here): `BILLING_CHARGE_NOT_PAYABLE`, `BILLING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED`, `BILLING_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE` — each added to `BillingErrorCode` and to both `errors.json` files (M26 rule, Wave 2 note); `docs/ERROR_CATALOG.md` § `BILLING_*` already lists all three, so no catalog edit.

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/application/use-cases/get-or-regenerate-charge-payment-link.use-case.ts`, `record-payment-return.use-case.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`, `.integration.spec.ts`) (new)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/controllers/billing.controller.ts` (+ specs), `apps/backend/http/billing/billing.http` (modify)
- `apps/bff/src/features/billing/billing.controller.ts`, `billing.schemas.ts`, `billing.types.ts`, `billing.mapper.ts` (+ specs), `apps/bff/http/billing/billing.http` (modify)
- `packages/types/src/billing.dto.ts` (modify)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/domain/charge.aggregate.ts` (+ spec) (modify — S02 already has `recordPaymentReturn(now)`; add the link-renewal behaviour, e.g. `renewPaymentLink(paymentUrl, expiresAt)`, which S02 does not have)
- `apps/backend/src/contexts/billing/infrastructure/http/billing-error.mapper.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`) (modify — `BILLING_CHARGE_NOT_PAYABLE`, `BILLING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED`, `BILLING_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE`, `error-mapper-coverage`)
- `packages/types/src/error-codes.ts` (modify — the three `BillingErrorCode` members), `packages/i18n/locales/pt-BR/errors.json` and `packages/i18n/locales/en/errors.json` (modify — the three entries), `apps/web/shared/lib/i18n/error-codes-exhaustiveness.spec.ts` (modify only if the spec needs it)
- `apps/bff/src/shared/http/problem-detail.ts` (modify — `BffThrowableCode` only for a code the BFF itself throws; none expected here)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] The manager gets a working pay link for any open charge (including an expired one and the recovery charge), pays, returns, and sees "pagamento recebido, reativando…" until the payment lands.
- [ ] A charge that cannot be paid any more says so; one that is already paid just refreshes to paid.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit:
  - [ ] Each of A1–A5; `payment_return_seen_at` set once and idempotent
  - [ ] The refresh failing (provider unreachable) still returns the unchanged state without an error
- Integration:
  - [ ] End to end on the fake: expired link regenerated (fake `LINK_EXPIRED`), return recorded, refresh applies a paid fact through `ApplyChargeFactUseCase` and reactivates a `PAST_DUE` tenant
  - [ ] The provider calls (link regeneration, the on-demand refresh) are outside any transaction (`transactional-io` passes)
  - [ ] A sweep-held recovery charge (incomplete address) → `BILLING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED`, no provider call; a `PENDING`-creation charge with no provider id (not held) → `BILLING_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE`, no provider call
  - [ ] **Race:** a webhook marking the charge `PAID` between the provider call and the link store is not overwritten (status stays `PAID`, link stored); the same for `payment-return` racing a webhook
  - [ ] Backend routes carry `@NoFeatureGate()` + `@SuspensionAccess(MANAGER_BILLING)` (decorator spec); the BFF routes carry no annotation (M25-S33)
  - [ ] Another tenant's charge id → not found; a `STAFF` token → `403`
- Tenant isolation: [ ] a manager can only generate links for and mark returns on their own tenant's charges
- E2E: none — covered by integration; the pay flow E2E is S35
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean
