## Wave 4 — Frontend

> **Shared frontend rules for every Wave 4 story** (cited once): the web slice is `apps/web/features/billing/{api,components,hooks,model}/` (created by the first story that needs it, S27); routes live under `apps/web/app/dashboard/billing/` and `app/[slug]/…`, kept thin (CLAUDE.md §7 Testing); server data uses `bffServerFetch` (authenticated, server-only) and client mutations / polling use `bffClient` through React Query hooks (precedent: `apps/web/features/booking/api/availability-alerts.server.ts` / `availability-alerts.ts`); `useTenant()` is the only source of `tenantId` in hooks; **never a raw `fetch()`** and never `--ba-*` in dashboard components (Tailwind + shadcn only); `ls apps/web/shared/components/ui/` and reuse what exists (`section-card`, `badge`, `button`, `alert-dialog`, `select`, `switch-field`, `pill-select`, `card`, `tabs`, and the `notice` primitive S27 adds) before writing a control; every new component ships its `.spec.tsx` in the same commit; every visible string uses `useTranslations()` with keys in **both** `packages/i18n/locales/pt-BR/web.json` and `…/en/web.json` (and every backend error code the UI maps is added with its translations by the backend story that first throws it (M25-S06 translated only `SIGNUP_TAX_ID_REQUIRED` and `SIGNUP_TAX_ID_INVALID`; `SIGNUP_TERMS_NOT_ACCEPTED` also exists already, and `BILLING_IDENTITY_TAX_ID_INVALID` is in neither `error-codes.ts` nor the locales until S16 adds it; see decision M6)); Playwright specs are test cases only, reusable flows live in `apps/web/e2e/helpers/billing/`. **Cross-cutting Wave 4 rules (decisions R6, R9):** (1) one shared summary query key `['billing','summary',tenantId]` (tenant from `useTenant()`), used by the Resumo, the tabs and the S32 banner; **every billing mutation** (pay, identity save, plan save/confirm, cancel, reactivate) invalidates it (`docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § A mutation that changes server state owned by another feature must invalidate that feature's query keys), each with a spy-based unit test. (2) **Money and dates:** every `*Minor` amount is converted to major units before `formatMoney(amount, locale, currency)`, with `currency = summary.currency` (never the tenant's display currency); date-only values (`dueDate`, `nextDueDate`, `effectiveFrom`, `YYYY-MM-DD`) are parsed with `utcDateFromISO` (`shared/lib/formatting/calendar-day.ts`), never `new Date('YYYY-MM-DD')`; instants (`trialEndsAt`, `suspendsAt`, `cancelAt`, `purgeAt`) are formatted in the **subscription timezone** (`summary.timezone`, S14), not the settings timezone. (3) **Retention copy (R6):** no screen promises erasure or warning emails before M27 — use "Seus dados são mantidos por pelo menos {90|180} dias; depois dessa data (a partir de {data}) podem ser apagados", with `purgeAt` always labelled as the earliest date; where no `purgeAt` exists (cancel pages) the generic wording "pelo menos 90 dias (180 se já houve pagamento)" is used. (4) **Form feedback:** saves report through `ActionPane`, `FeedbackMessage`, `FieldFeedback` and `shared/lib/feedback/use-form-feedback` (`docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § A form's error or success message lives in the action pane); public forms use `shared/components/hotsite/branded-feedback-message`; never a hand-written `role="alert"`. (5) Error messages come from `resolveApiErrorFeedback` on the error `code`, never from `detail`. The Wave 4 stories share `packages/i18n/locales/*/web.json` and the billing page files and are therefore **sequential in practice** (see `## Run-batch preview`).

### M26-S27 — Billing page shell, Resumo and Cobranças tabs (UC-117)

**Agent:** `frontend-ts`
**Complexity:** L
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-117 (all, A1–A8), `docs/DASHBOARD_FRONTEND_ARCHITECTURE.md` § Staff/Manager Shell (the planned Assinatura entry), `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Manager API (summary and charge history), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § Reuse the repo's UI primitives, `docs/CODE_STANDARDS.md`, `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_SHARED.md` § Authoring new i18n UI copy keys, `docs/BFF_ARCHITECTURE.md` § Web → BFF Transport Layer, `journeys/manager/billing.md`
**Dependencies:** M26-S14 (summary and charges reads), M25-S29 (the route-to-feature registry the nav entry uses, and the `/dashboard/suspended` manager notice page, which this page **supplements** — S32 keeps that page for a manager who opens the URL directly; M25-S31a only adds the staff variant)
**Pattern:** Server Component fetch (`bffServerFetch`) hydrating the shared summary query (`['billing','summary',tenantId]`) with client sub-components for tabs; one shared `generateMetadata()` fetch via React `cache()`; tabs as a `?tab=` query parameter (`resumo|plano|cobrancas|dados|automatica`, the journey's contract, also the deep-link target of S30/S32) on the existing `apps/web/shared/components/ui/tabs.tsx` primitive (it exists — read it first; a billing-local `role="tablist"` bar following the `ServiceEditTabBar` precedent is justified in the PR only if `ui/tabs` cannot be driven by a controlled `?tab=` value).

**Description:**
Build the manager's "Assinatura" page (`/dashboard/billing`): one page, five tabs and one primary action per tab — **Resumo**, **Plano** (S30), **Cobranças**, **Dados de cobrança** (S29) and **Cobrança automática** (shown as "Em breve" until M29; no endpoint). Destructive actions (cancel, S31) are separate confirmation pages. This story builds the shell, the nav entry and the two read tabs. **Resumo** shows the status, trial end or next due date, the active features with tier and price, the total, the open charge with the **Pagar agora** call to action (wired in S28), the automatic-billing status, announced price changes, the cancel link and "Falar com a gente"; every state in `journeys/manager/prototypes/billing/` (01–01o) is covered: loading skeleton and load error with retry (A1), trial, active, past-due, suspended (reason, the open recovery charge and the earliest possible clean-up date — labelled as such, not a scheduled deletion, since no job acts on it before M27; A5), suspended-canceled, suspended-operator, payment-reversed, no-charges (A2), price-change notice, **exempt** ("Conta isenta de cobrança" on every tab, no actions — A7) and the trial "Confirmar plano" prompt with the automatic-invoice date (A8); a staff or customer opening the route gets the 403 / redirect (A6). **Cobranças** is the paginated history (subtotal, total, status, method, receipt link on paid charges; BR has no tax lines). The "Pagamento recebido, reativando…" polling state (A3) is S28's. The nav entry "Assinatura" is **manager-only** (one data-driven entry in `DASHBOARD_NAV`, `apps/web/shells/dashboard/model/dashboard-nav.ts`: group `manager`, roles `MANAGER`, surfaces sidebar and sheet, no bottom tab; the nav is data-driven, so `Sidebar.tsx` and `MoreSheet.tsx` render it with no per-item edit) and is registered in the M25-S29 route-to-feature registry as a deliberately ungated route (the backend counterpart is `@NoFeatureGate`) so it stays reachable while suspended; its label is the new key `dashboard.nav.billing` (the `DashboardNavLabelKey` union gains `nav.billing`; `dashboard-nav.spec.ts` asserts the entry for `MANAGER` only), and `BottomNav.tsx` only needs the drill-down hide for `/dashboard/billing/cancel`, no new item. **Shell wiring (every dashboard section needs it):** a new `app/dashboard/billing/layout.tsx` calling `loadDashboardShellContext` and rendering `DashboardSectionShell` (without it the page has no Locale/Tenant providers); `/dashboard/billing` added to `MANAGER_ONLY_ROUTES` in `proxy.ts` (+ `proxy.spec.ts`) so STAFF is redirected to `/dashboard` (A6 — `proxy.ts` is modified for this one line only; the suspended redirect is never in `proxy.ts`); `shells/dashboard/model/topbar-route.ts` `PAGE_TITLE_KEYS` gains the billing title and the cancel page's back link (the file has **no spec today**, so `topbar-route.spec.ts` is new). **Decided at the M26 audit (2026-11-18) — shell plumbing, verified against the code:** `DashboardShellContext` carries only `lockedFeatures` and `routeFeature` today (no suspended flag; the M25 access answer is read inside `loadDashboardShellContext` and not kept), and `resolveDashboardNav` takes `{ role, lockedFeatures, toggles }`. This story adds the **role and operational/suspended state** to the shell: `DashboardShellContext` gains `operational: boolean` (false when the access answer says the tenant is suspended), `DashboardShell.tsx` passes it and the role down, `resolveDashboardNav` accepts it (the disabled entries themselves are S32's), and `resolveSuspendedRedirect` gains a `role` input (S32 uses it). **Fail-soft settings read for a suspended manager (blocker found at the audit):** once a suspended manager is sent to `/dashboard/billing`, the billing `layout.tsx` calls `loadDashboardShellContext`, which calls `fetchTenantSettings` — it throws on a non-OK answer and `GET /tenants/settings` is `@SuspensionAccess('DEFAULT_DENY')` (`403` for a suspended tenant; M25's `/dashboard/suspended` sits outside the shell for exactly this reason). So `loadDashboardShellContext` reads the settings **fail-soft**: on a `403` for a suspended tenant it falls back to the JWT locale and, for currency and timezone, to `summary.currency` / `summary.timezone` (the billing page already formats from the summary, Wave 4 rule 2), and the lead-form status read is skipped. A spec covers a suspended access answer plus a `403` settings read (the layout renders, no throw, no redirect loop). The Plano and Dados tabs render a neutral "em breve" placeholder until S30 and S29 replace them (Wave 4 is sequential). The loading skeleton is written with the repo's inline `animate-pulse` blocks (no skeleton primitive exists in `ui/`). **Resumo also covers** the scheduled-cancel state `06b` (ACTIVE with `cancelAt`: grey alert "Cancelada — acesso até dd/mm", badge "Cancelando", cancel link hidden; the "Reativar assinatura" action is mounted by S31), the interim discard line when `canDiscard` is false ("Para descartar este teste, fale com a gente"), and the retention line per the Wave 4 copy rule. **"Falar com a gente"** links to the owner tenant's hotsite booking page: `/{PLATFORM_OWNER_TENANT_SLUG}/booking`, with the slug read server-side from the web env introduced by S33 (S27 renders the link from a shared helper `getTalkToUsHref()` that S33 backs with the env; until S33 lands it falls back to hiding the link). Money, dates and the shared summary key follow the Wave 4 rules above.

**Notice primitive (decided at the M26 audit, 2026-11-18).** The trial, past-due, payment-received, cancelled and exempt notices have no primitive: `ui/feedback-message.tsx` offers only `error | success`, and a hand-written `role="alert"` / `role="status"` is an ESLint error (the prototype `04b` draws an amber alert). This story adds a `ui/` notice primitive `apps/web/shared/components/ui/notice.tsx` (+ `notice.spec.tsx`) with variants `info | warning | neutral`, **no ARIA role** (static page content, not a live region), reused by S28 and S32; a spec asserts the primitive renders no `role="alert"` / `role="status"`. Errors and save results still use `FeedbackMessage`.

**Page-level action pane (decided at the M26 audit).** The prototype has **one primary action per tab** in a sticky aside (>= 1024px) and a bottom bar on mobile that swaps per tab: Salvar alterações (Dados), Salvar plano / Confirmar plano (Plano), Pagar agora (Resumo), Reativar assinatura, plus "Descartar alterações", the live "Novo total mensal" box and an amber dirty dot on the tab label. The shell hosts it the way `ServiceEditPage` does: a page-level `ActionPane` host in `BillingPage`/`BillingTabs` and each tab registers its action, dirty state and feedback through `useRegisterTabAction` / `useTabFeedback` (`apps/web/features/booking/components/dashboard/services/service-tab-action.ts` is the precedent; promoting it to a shared location is a story-discovery call, since it would be a cross-slice import otherwise) with `MOBILE_ACTION_BAR_CLEARANCE_CLASS` for the mobile bar (BottomNav stays visible on billing). S29 and S30 register their tabs on this host; labels: Dados "Salvar alterações", Plano "Salvar plano" (during the trial "Confirmar plano").

**Cobranças details (verified against `dev-notes.md`).** Columns Vencimento / Referência / Valor / Situação (2-line cards on mobile); the history includes `REFUNDED` and `CHARGEBACK` rows; pager "1–5 de 8 cobranças" with Anterior/Próxima, page size 5 (`limit=5`); the footnote "Reembolsos e contestações são tratados pelo provedor de pagamento…". The **Referência** value of a `RECOVERY` charge is not drawn in the prototype — **OPEN: decide at `/story-discovery`** (the service period for a `PERIOD` charge, a "Reativação" label for a recovery charge). The receipt link is "still to draw" in the journey, so the paid row shows the plain link to `receiptUrl` with a story-discovery check on its label. Each tab has its own loading and fetch-error state (retry), not only Dados.

**US variant (documented non-goal).** The prototype also draws a US variant (four tabs without "Cobrança automática", the "+ imposto calculado na cobrança" label; `journeys/manager/prototypes/billing/dev-notes.md`, `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Manager API). With signup BR-only (S23 `SIGNUP_SUPPORTED_COUNTRIES`) and all US behaviour in M28, this story renders the BR shape only (five tabs, tax-inclusive labels); the US shape is a non-goal until M28.

**Prototype references:** `journeys/manager/prototypes/billing/01-billing-active.html`, `01b`–`01o`, `01q` (annual is M30 — not built), `journeys/manager/billing.md`.
**BFF endpoint spec:** none new — consumes `GET /v1/billing/summary` and `GET /v1/billing/charges` (S14).
**Backend HTTP surface:** none — consumes S14.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** i18n keys under `dashboard.billing.*` in both locales.

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/web/app/dashboard/billing/page.tsx`, `layout.tsx`, `loading.tsx`, `error.tsx` (+ `error.spec.tsx`) (new — thin)
- `apps/web/features/billing/api/billing.server.ts`, `billing.ts` (+ `.spec.ts`) (new)
- `apps/web/features/billing/components/BillingPage.tsx`, `BillingTabs.tsx`, `BillingSummaryTab.tsx`, `BillingChargesTab.tsx`, `BillingStatusBadge.tsx`, `AutoBillingComingSoon.tsx`, `ExemptNotice.tsx` (+ `.spec.tsx` each) (new)
- `apps/web/features/billing/hooks/useBillingSummary.ts`, `useBillingCharges.ts` (+ specs) (new)
- `apps/web/features/billing/model/billing-view-model.ts` (+ spec) (new — pure mapping of summary → view state, node-env Vitest)
- `apps/web/shells/dashboard/model/dashboard-nav.ts` (+ `dashboard-nav.spec.ts`) (modify — the manager-only `nav.billing` entry in `DASHBOARD_NAV`, the `DashboardNavLabelKey` union, and the operational/suspended input to `resolveDashboardNav`)
- `apps/web/shells/dashboard/model/dashboard-shell-context.ts` (+ `dashboard-shell-context.spec.ts`) (modify — `operational`/suspended state kept from the access answer, and the fail-soft settings read for a suspended manager)
- `apps/web/shells/dashboard/components/DashboardShell.tsx` (+ spec) (modify — passes role and the operational state down to the nav)
- `apps/web/shared/lib/billing/suspended-redirect.ts` (+ spec) (modify — a `role` input; the target change is S32's)
- `apps/web/shells/dashboard/components/BottomNav.tsx` (+ spec) (modify — hides on `/dashboard/billing/cancel`); `Sidebar.tsx` and `MoreSheet.tsx` need no edit (data-driven), their specs gain a MANAGER-only assertion for the entry
- `apps/web/shared/components/ui/notice.tsx` (+ `notice.spec.tsx`) (new — `info | warning | neutral`, no ARIA role)
- `apps/web/features/billing/components/BillingActionPane.tsx`, `apps/web/features/billing/model/billing-tab-action.ts` (+ specs) (new — the page-level action pane host and per-tab registration, dirty dot)
- `apps/web/shells/dashboard/model/topbar-route.ts` (modify — billing page title, cancel page back link), `apps/web/shells/dashboard/model/topbar-route.spec.ts` (new — the file has no spec today)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A manager sees the right Resumo for every state in the prototype (trial, active, past-due, suspended in its three reasons, exempt, no charges, price change, plan unconfirmed) and the full paginated history with receipt links.
- [ ] The page is reachable while the tenant is suspended and unreachable for staff and customers; "Cobrança automática" says "Em breve".
- [ ] No state shows another tenant's data or a suspension reason to a non-manager.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit (Vitest):
  - [ ] `billing-view-model` per status (table-driven); each component renders its prototype state; i18n keys exist in both locales (the exhaustiveness test passes)
  - [ ] **Suspended manager on the billing layout:** with a suspended access answer and a `403` on the settings read, `loadDashboardShellContext` returns a context (JWT locale, currency/timezone from the summary) and does not throw or redirect; `DashboardShellContext` carries the role and the operational state
  - [ ] `ui/notice` renders `info | warning | neutral` with no `role="alert"` / `role="status"`; the billing tabs register their action, dirty state and feedback on the page-level action pane (the primary swaps per tab; a dirty tab shows the dot)
  - [ ] Cobranças: columns, page size 5, `REFUNDED` / `CHARGEBACK` rows, footnote, per-tab loading and fetch-error with retry
  - [ ] Nav entry rendered for `MANAGER` only (`dashboard-nav.spec.ts`); `proxy.spec.ts`: a STAFF token on `/dashboard/billing` and `/dashboard/billing/cancel` is redirected to `/dashboard`
  - [ ] Money (minor → major, `summary.currency`), date-only (`utcDateFromISO`, no one-day shift in `America/Sao_Paulo`) and instant (subscription timezone) formatting; the retention line uses the earliest-date wording; the summary hook uses the shared key
  - [ ] Negative guarantee, named test: a STAFF/CUSTOMER session never renders a suspension reason (the route is unreachable and `BillingPage` renders nothing without the MANAGER role)
- Integration: n/a (web)
- E2E:
  - [ ] Covered by S35's billing-page scenarios (Playwright) — **named here:** `billing-page-states.spec.ts` renders each seeded state (S13 seed) and asserts the visible status, primary action and, for exempt, the absence of actions
- [ ] `ls apps/web/shared/components/ui/` checked; no hand-rolled primitive; no `--ba-*` in the dashboard
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S28 — "Pagar agora" — payment link, return and polling (UC-119)

**Agent:** `frontend-ts`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-119 and UC-117 (A3, A4), `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Manager API (payment-link, payment-return), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § Reuse the repo's UI primitives, `docs/CODE_STANDARDS.md`, `journeys/manager/billing.md`
**Dependencies:** M26-S15 (the two endpoints), M26-S27 (the page and the Resumo tab)
**Pattern:** React Query mutation + a bounded polling query keyed on `paymentConfirmationPending`.

**Description:**
Wire the Resumo's primary action. **Pagar agora** calls `POST /v1/billing/charges/{chargeId}/payment-link` and navigates to the provider's hosted page; the `returnUrl` passed to `createCharge` at issue time (S05; Asaas `callback`, usable only if Asaas's callback-domain rule accepts the web host — verified in S37) brings the manager back to `/dashboard/billing?payment=return&charge={id}` (**S05 owns the `returnUrl`**: `${FRONTEND_URL}/dashboard/billing?payment=return&charge={chargeId}`, with its env source and spec, so the `charge` query parameter is the charge this story passes to `payment-return`; this story builds no URL itself; if the provider cannot honour a return URL, the click also stores `sessionStorage['billing.payReturn']` so a manual return to the page is recognised; a later visit with `paymentConfirmationPending` already true resumes polling without a new return call). **Return detection:** the page treats `?payment=return` (or the sessionStorage marker) as the return, **clears the sessionStorage marker and strips the query parameters before calling** `POST …/payment-return` once for the charge in the marker (decided at the M26 audit: clearing first means a reload during the call cannot fire it twice), then shows "pagamento recebido, reativando…" (`paymentConfirmationPending`), **polling `GET /v1/billing/summary` every 5 seconds for about a minute** (A3) until the status leaves pending or the window ends (then a calm "ainda processando" message with a manual refresh). Link expired / failed payment → a regenerate / retry affordance (A4: "Gerar novo link"); payment in processing (A2: Pix / boleto compensation) → "pagamento em processamento"; charge already paid → the page refreshes to the paid state; `BILLING_CHARGE_NOT_PAYABLE` → an explanation and a summary refresh. **Failure (`01o`, decided by editor):** the API gives no failure signal (payment-return answers only `{ status, paymentConfirmationPending }`), so `01o` is **not a distinct state**: a return whose charge is still open after the polling window shows the calm "ainda processando" message with "Tentar pagar novamente" (`payment-link` again) beside the manual refresh. **Other outcomes of S15's contract:** `BILLING_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE` on `payment-link` → `FeedbackMessage` above the open-charge card ("Não foi possível abrir a página de pagamento") with the button still enabled (`journeys/manager/prototypes/billing/dev-notes.md` "Specified but not drawn", row "Payment-link failure"); `BILLING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED` (a sweep-held recovery charge, decision R1) → the manager is sent to the Dados tab; `openCharge.hasPaymentLink = false` (charge not yet created at the provider) → the button is disabled with "estamos preparando o link" and the summary is refetched, never a payment-link call. States match `01i`, `01j`, `01c`, `01d`. After every pay/return/regenerate call the shared summary key is invalidated. While `SUSPENDED` the action pays the **recovery** charge. The polling stops when the page is hidden (visibility API) and never runs for exempt tenants.

**Prototype references:** `journeys/manager/prototypes/billing/01i-billing-payment-pending.html`, `01j-billing-payment-link-expired.html`, `01c-billing-past-due.html`, `01d-billing-suspended.html` (`01o-billing-payment-failed.html` is folded into the "ainda processando" state — see the Description; the prototype needs a one-line note, listed for the journeys owner).
**BFF endpoint spec:** none new — consumes S15's two routes and the summary.
**Backend HTTP surface:** none — consumes S15.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** i18n keys under `dashboard.billing.pay.*` in both locales.

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/web/features/billing/components/PayNowButton.tsx`, `PaymentPendingBanner.tsx`, `PaymentLinkExpiredNotice.tsx` (+ `.spec.tsx` each) (new)
- `apps/web/features/billing/utils/payment-return-marker.ts` (+ spec) (new — reads/clears `?payment=return` and the sessionStorage marker)
- `apps/web/features/billing/hooks/usePayCharge.ts`, `usePaymentConfirmationPolling.ts` (+ specs) (new)
- `apps/web/features/billing/api/billing.ts` (+ spec) (modify)
- `apps/web/features/billing/components/BillingSummaryTab.tsx` (+ spec) (modify)
- `packages/i18n/locales/pt-BR/web.json`, `packages/i18n/locales/en/web.json` (modify)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A manager can pay any open charge (period or recovery) on the provider's page, returns, sees the pending state and then the paid state without reloading; an expired link is regenerated; a failed payment can be retried.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit (Vitest):
  - [ ] Polling: 5 s cadence, stops after the window, stops when hidden, stops on a paid status (fake timers)
  - [ ] Each of A2–A5 renders its state (the banners and notices use the `ui/notice` primitive S27 adds, never a hand-written role); the return call is made exactly once and only when the marker is present; the marker is cleared **before** the call (a simulated reload mid-call makes no second call); no marker and `paymentConfirmationPending` false → no call
  - [ ] `BILLING_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE`, `BILLING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED` and `hasPaymentLink = false` each render their state; the summary key is invalidated after each call
- E2E:
  - [ ] Covered by S35 — **named here:** `billing-pay-flow.spec.ts` (fake provider: create the link, simulate `PAID_PIX`, assert the page leaves the pending state)
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S29 — "Dados de cobrança" tab (UC-118)

**Agent:** `frontend-ts`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-118 (all), `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Manager API (identity), `docs/VALUE_OBJECTS_REFERENCE.md` (`CountrySpec`, `Address`), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § Reuse the repo's UI primitives, `docs/CODE_STANDARDS.md`, `journeys/manager/billing.md`
**Dependencies:** M26-S16 (identity endpoints), M26-S27 (the page and tabs)
**Pattern:** Country-driven form rendered from `CountrySpec`; reuse of the CEP lookup hook; React Hook Form + Zod mirroring the BFF schema.

**Description:**
The identity tab. The form is **rendered from the billing country's `CountrySpec`** — labels, placeholders, formats and fields follow the country in the tenant's language (BR: Logradouro, Número, Complemento, Bairro, Cidade, UF, CEP with ViaCEP autofill through the existing `useAddressLookup` hook, **promoted to `apps/web/shared/hooks/useAddressLookup.ts`** by this story (decided at the M26 audit, 2026-11-18: it lives in `features/booking/hooks/` today with two booking consumers; billing is the third consumer, and a cross-slice import into another domain's hooks is what the UI layer model forbids, so the hook moves to `shared/` and its two importers, `features/booking/components/public/AddressFields.tsx` and `features/booking/components/dashboard/bookings/new-booking/NewBookingAddressFields.tsx`, are updated in the same commit; the adapter itself is already the shared module-level singleton `viaCepAddressLookup` in `shared/lib/address/`, so the billing form imports it the same way, with no new instantiation); US: Street, Number, Apt/Suite, City, State, ZIP Code, no neighbourhood). Fields: tax-id type (`CNPJ` / `CPF` / `EIN` / `OTHER`) and number (**BR: always present, editable, never blankable** — S16 allows the PUT to supply a missing BR tax id, "never blankable" means an existing one cannot be cleared; an `exempt` account is not required to have one, so an empty tax id there is neither an error nor a blocker; the US variant (optional tax id with `EIN` or `OTHER`, "Street / Apt/Suite / ZIP Code" labels — A3) is a **documented non-goal until M28**: signup is BR-only (S23), so no US tenant exists to see it, and the form is built country-driven from `CountrySpec` so M28 adds it without a rewrite), legal name, billing email, address. The hook takes a `HotsiteAddressSpec`, so `model/identity-form.ts` maps the billing country's `CountrySpec.address` to that shape (a pure, spec-covered mapping). Client validation mirrors the BFF schema, including the **alphanumeric CNPJ**: the check-digit rule accepts digits and uppercase letters in the first twelve positions (`^[0-9A-Z]{12}\d{2}$`, as the backend `TaxId` does, case-insensitive input normalised to uppercase) alongside the CPF rule; server errors map to fields (`BILLING_IDENTITY_TAX_ID_INVALID`, `BILLING_IDENTITY_EMAIL_INVALID`, postal/state format — A1, A2, A5); save failure keeps the form (A4); success shows the saved state (`02d`) in the action pane. Save, field errors and the form-level error go through `ActionPane` / `useFormFeedback` / `FieldFeedback` (the first invalid field is focused); the tab registers "Salvar alterações" and its dirty state on the page-level action pane host S27 builds (amber dot on the tab label while dirty, "Cancelar" reverts to the loaded values); a successful save invalidates the shared summary key so `addressRequired`, the S32 banner and the Plano tab update without a reload. Between the Resumo and this tab, a loading skeleton and a tab-level `FeedbackMessage` with retry cover a failed identity read. While the full address is missing (`summary.addressRequired`), the tab shows the required-address state (`02e` / `02f`) and a note that it unlocks the first paid plan selection. The tab is read/write even while suspended.

**Prototype references:** `journeys/manager/prototypes/billing/02-billing-identity-br.html`, `02b-billing-identity-br-erro.html`, `02c-billing-identity-us.html`, `02d-billing-identity-saved.html`, `02e-billing-identity-us-address-required.html`, `02f-billing-identity-br-address-required.html`.
**BFF endpoint spec:** none new — consumes S16's `GET`/`PUT /v1/billing/identity`.
**Backend HTTP surface:** none — consumes S16.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** i18n keys under `dashboard.billing.identity.*` in both locales.

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/web/features/billing/components/BillingIdentityTab.tsx`, `BillingIdentityForm.tsx`, `AddressRequiredNotice.tsx` (+ `.spec.tsx` each) (new)
- `apps/web/features/billing/hooks/useBillingIdentity.ts`, `useUpdateBillingIdentity.ts` (+ specs) (new)
- `apps/web/features/billing/model/identity-form.ts` (+ spec) (new — Zod schema, alphanumeric CNPJ / CPF check-digit rules and the `CountrySpec.address` → `HotsiteAddressSpec` mapping)
- `apps/web/shared/hooks/useAddressLookup.ts` (+ spec) (new location — moved from `apps/web/features/booking/hooks/useAddressLookup.ts`, which is deleted), `apps/web/features/booking/components/public/AddressFields.tsx`, `apps/web/features/booking/components/dashboard/bookings/new-booking/NewBookingAddressFields.tsx` (+ specs) (modify — import path only)
- `apps/web/features/billing/api/billing.ts` (+ spec) (modify)
- `apps/web/features/billing/components/BillingTabs.tsx` (+ spec) (modify — mounts the tab, replacing the placeholder)
- `packages/i18n/locales/pt-BR/web.json`, `packages/i18n/locales/en/web.json` (modify)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A BR manager edits the legal name, email, CPF/CNPJ (never blank) and the full address with CEP autofill; the form shows country-correct labels and errors in the tenant's language; saving confirms; a missing address is flagged until saved.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit (Vitest):
  - [ ] Field set and labels per country spec (BR and US); BR tax-id blanking blocked; invalid CNPJ/CPF and email show field errors; server error codes mapped to fields
  - [ ] ViaCEP autofill fills the address through the shared `useAddressLookup`; the booking forms still pass their specs after the move; an alphanumeric CNPJ (`12ABC34501DE35`) validates and a wrong check digit fails; save failure keeps the form
  - [ ] A successful save invalidates `['billing','summary',tenantId]` (spy); a feedback message renders in the action pane, never as a hand-written `role="alert"`
- E2E:
  - [ ] Covered by S35 — **named here:** `billing-identity.spec.ts` (BR happy path with the address, then the plan tab accepts a selection)
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S30 — "Plano" tab and "Confirmar plano" (UC-136)

**Agent:** `frontend-ts`
**Complexity:** L
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-136 (all, A1–A13), UC-130, `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Manager API (plan routes), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § Reuse the repo's UI primitives, `docs/CODE_STANDARDS.md`, `journeys/manager/billing.md`
**Dependencies:** M26-S17 (catalog and preview), M26-S19 (save and confirm), M26-S21 (the chatbot tier mapping — the Plus tier must be real before it is sold), M26-S27 (the page)
**Pattern:** Controlled form with a debounced preview query; one primary action whose label depends on the trial state; saves report through `ActionPane` / `useFormFeedback` and invalidate the shared summary key (`planConfirmationRequired`, the S32 banner).

**Description:**
The plan tab lists every feature with its price for the tenant's country, a toggle, the chatbot tier choice (Básico / Plus), dependency hints and a **live total**; each change is previewed with its effective date ("entra em dd/mm" / "sai em dd/mm") through `POST /v1/billing/plan/preview` (debounced on every change). One primary action: **Salvar plano** (`PUT /v1/billing/plan`), which during the trial is labelled **Confirmar plano** and also sets `plan_confirmed_at` (S19), so confirming the untouched default is the same `PUT` (A13 → "já confirmado" on `BILLING_PLAN_ALREADY_CONFIRMED`); the UI never calls `POST /v1/billing/plan/confirm`. States match the prototype: edit (`08`), trial choose (`08b`), dependency error (`08c`), saved (`08d`), a payment-suspended tenant choosing its plan before paying (`08e`, UC-136 A14: editable, the preview says "vale a partir do pagamento" and shows the recovery-charge total, "Salvar plano" re-issues the recovery charge, and the tab turns read-only again only on the "pagamento recebido, reativando…" state `01i`), past due (`08f`, read-only), an operator suspension (`01n`, read-only) or cancelling (A4, read-only), scheduled next-period selection (`08g` — "Alterações agendadas para dd/mm", current and scheduled selections side by side, replaceable until the next invoice is paid; there is no first-billed lock), save error (`08h`), BOOKING in use (`08i` — names what blocks the removal, A11), plan-confirm required (`09`). A newly selected feature is described as starting at the next paid period ("começa em dd/mm"), never as instant (the locked-feature screens say the same); a paid next invoice defers the change to the following renewal and says so (A7, A12); an unavailable price shows the feature as unavailable (A6); the missing-address rejection links to the Dados tab (A10). `BILLING_FEATURE_*` errors surface their specific messages and the selection is kept (A9). `BILLING_FEATURE_IN_USE` names what blocks the removal from the response `params` S18/S19 define (counts per category — bookings, recurring schedules, availability alerts — and the effective date), rendered as `08i` does. A dated list-price change on a feature (`upcoming` per item in `GET /v1/billing/plan`, S17) shows the read-only note "Novo valor a partir de dd/mm: R$ …" (`01k`). **Locked-feature buy actions (decided at the M26 audit, 2026-11-18).** The M25 locked screens ("Contratar este recurso") carry three actions per the journey dev-notes: **Contratar este recurso** (→ `/dashboard/billing?tab=plano`), **Ver minha assinatura** (→ `/dashboard/billing`) and **Falar com a gente** (via S27's `getTalkToUsHref()`). The markup lives in `LockedFeatureCard.tsx` (the `page` and `section` variants; used by `LockedFeaturePage.tsx` and directly by the settings and hotsite pages), **not** in `LockedFeaturePage.tsx`, so this story edits the card and both variants. The actions render **only for the MANAGER role** (a STAFF member would get a dead link: `/dashboard/billing` is manager-only, S27); staff keep the plain locked notice. The primary action on the Plano tab is labelled **Salvar plano** (during the trial **Confirmar plano**), matching the prototype's page-level action pane that S27 builds; the tab registers it, "Descartar alterações" and the live "Novo total mensal" box there. **Confirmar plano with no change** always calls `PUT /v1/billing/plan` (decided: one path; the API contract allows `POST /plan/confirm` too, but a single call keeps the UI and the tests to one code path; the "already confirmed" answer is the backend's `BILLING_PLAN_ALREADY_CONFIRMED`). **US variant:** the "+ imposto calculado na cobrança" label and the four-tab shape are a documented non-goal until M28 (BR-only signup, S23). Prices use the Wave 4 money rule.

**Prototype references:** `journeys/manager/prototypes/billing/08-plan-edit.html`, `08b`–`08i`, `09-plan-confirm-required.html`.
**BFF endpoint spec:** none new — consumes S17 and S19.
**Backend HTTP surface:** none — consumes S17 and S19.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** i18n keys under `dashboard.billing.plan.*` in both locales.

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/web/features/billing/components/BillingPlanTab.tsx`, `PlanFeatureRow.tsx`, `PlanTierSelect.tsx`, `PlanTotalBar.tsx`, `PlanChangePreview.tsx`, `PlanRemovalBlockedNotice.tsx`, `ConfirmPlanPrompt.tsx` (+ `.spec.tsx` each) (new)
- `apps/web/features/billing/hooks/useBillingPlan.ts`, `usePlanPreview.ts`, `useSavePlan.ts`, `useConfirmPlan.ts` (+ specs) (new)
- `apps/web/features/billing/model/plan-selection.ts` (+ spec) (new — pure selection / dependency hints)
- `apps/web/features/billing/components/BillingTabs.tsx` (+ spec) (modify — mounts the tab)
- `apps/web/features/billing/components/LockedFeatureCard.tsx` (+ `LockedFeatureCard.spec.tsx`) (modify — the three buy actions in both the `page` and `section` variants, MANAGER only; M25-S29 created it); `apps/web/features/billing/components/LockedFeaturePage.tsx` (+ spec) (modify only if it must pass the role)
- `apps/web/features/billing/api/billing.ts` (+ spec) (modify)
- `packages/i18n/locales/pt-BR/web.json`, `packages/i18n/locales/en/web.json` (modify)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A manager toggles features and the chatbot tier, sees what each change does and when, and saves; during the trial the same action confirms the first invoice's content; impossible selections are explained.
- [ ] No state implies instant access for a newly added feature.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit (Vitest):
  - [ ] Each prototype state (`08`–`09`) renders; preview is debounced; the primary label switches in trial; read-only states (past due, operator suspension, cancelling, exempt, payment in progress) disable the controls and the payment-suspended states (`01d`, `01e`, `01m`, `08e`) stay editable
  - [ ] Error-code → message mapping for every `BILLING_*` plan code; `BILLING_FEATURE_IN_USE` renders the categories from `params`
  - [ ] Save and confirm invalidate `['billing','summary',tenantId]` (spy); the `upcoming` note renders; `LockedFeatureCard` (both variants) shows **Contratar este recurso** (`?tab=plano`), **Ver minha assinatura** and **Falar com a gente** for a MANAGER and none of them for STAFF; confirming an untouched trial plan makes one `PUT` and no `POST …/confirm`
- E2E:
  - [ ] Covered by S35 — **named here:** `billing-plan.spec.ts` (toggle a feature, preview, save; trial confirm; removal blocked by a live booking)
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S31 — Cancel and reactivate pages (UC-133, UC-134)

**Agent:** `frontend-ts`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-133 and UC-134 (all), `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Manager API (cancel, reactivate), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § Reuse the repo's UI primitives, `docs/CODE_STANDARDS.md`, `journeys/manager/billing.md`
**Dependencies:** M26-S20 (cancel / reactivate endpoints), M26-S27 (the page)
**Pattern:** Destructive actions on their own confirmation page (`alert-dialog` for the second confirm where the prototype uses one); status-driven wording.

**Description:**
Cancel is a **separate confirmation page** reached from the Resumo's "Cancelar assinatura" link: the wording depends on the status — `ACTIVE`: access continues to dd/mm, nothing refunded (`06`); `TRIALING` with the first invoice paid: access continues to the end of the paid period (`cancelAt`, decision R5; `06e`); `PAST_DUE` / unpaid trial: immediate suspension with the explicit note that nothing was paid (`06d`, A3). **Decided at the M26 audit (2026-11-18):** the page reads `summary.firstChargePaid`, the boolean S14 adds to the summary (true once the first `PERIOD` charge is `PAID`, and it stays true after a later cancel), so the `TRIALING` wording is chosen **before** the click: `firstChargePaid` true → the scheduled wording (`06e`, access to the end of the paid period), false → the immediate wording with the explicit "nothing was paid" note (`06d`). Why: without the flag the page could only hedge ("se a primeira fatura já foi paga…"), which contradicts UC-133 A3's requirement to state exactly what will happen; one boolean on a read that already exists is the least machinery. The outcome screen (`06b` or `06d`, from the response `cancelAt`) stays authoritative if the two ever disagree (a payment landing between the read and the click). **Data paragraph (decision R6):** "Seus dados são mantidos por pelo menos 90 dias (180 se já houve pagamento); depois disso podem ser apagados. Você pode reativar pagando 1 mês." — never "apagados definitivamente" or "avisos 30 e 7 dias" (no purge job or warning email exists before M27); the prototype copy of `06`/`06d`/`06e` is superseded. Outcomes: scheduled (`06b` "Cancelada — acesso até dd/mm") with an "Desfazer cancelamento" action; immediate (`06d`); already cancelling (`409` → shows the scheduled state, A1); error with retry (`06c`, A4). **Reactivate**: "Reativar assinatura" for a scheduled cancel or a `CANCELED` suspension; for a payment-based suspension the Resumo already shows "Pague 1 mês para reativar" with the recovery charge's **Pagar agora** (S28). `POST /v1/billing/reactivate` returns the open charge; the page then calls `payment-link` (reactivate never returns a URL) — a provider failure shows `BILLING_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE` with retry (A3); a missing billing address answers `BILLING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED` with nothing created (decision R1) and sends the manager to the Dados tab (A6). Cancel, undo and reactivate invalidate the shared summary key. An `OPERATOR` suspension shows no reactivate action (only the operator lifts it, `01n`). The annual wording (`13`) is M30.

**Prototype references:** `journeys/manager/prototypes/billing/06-cancel-confirm.html`, `06b-cancel-scheduled.html`, `06c-cancel-error.html`, `06d-cancel-immediate.html`, `06e-cancel-trial-paid.html`, `01e-billing-suspended-canceled.html`, `01n-billing-suspended-operator.html`.
**BFF endpoint spec:** none new — consumes S20.
**Backend HTTP surface:** none — consumes S20.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** i18n keys under `dashboard.billing.cancel.*` and `…reactivate.*` in both locales.

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/web/app/dashboard/billing/cancel/page.tsx` (new — thin)
- `apps/web/features/billing/components/CancelSubscriptionPage.tsx`, `CancelOutcome.tsx`, `ReactivateAction.tsx` (+ `.spec.tsx` each) (new)
- `apps/web/features/billing/components/BillingSummaryTab.tsx`, `BillingStatusBadge.tsx` (+ specs) (modify — mounts `ReactivateAction` and the scheduled-cancel state `06b`/badge "Cancelando"; the cancel link)
- `apps/web/shared/lib/billing/feature-registry.ts` (+ spec) (modify — `/dashboard/billing/cancel` as an ungated route; M25-S29's spec asserts every dashboard page is mapped)
- `apps/web/features/billing/hooks/useCancelSubscription.ts`, `useReactivateSubscription.ts` (+ specs) (new)
- `apps/web/features/billing/api/billing.ts` (+ spec) (modify)
- `packages/i18n/locales/pt-BR/web.json`, `packages/i18n/locales/en/web.json` (modify)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A manager can cancel with wording that matches exactly what will happen (scheduled vs immediate, nothing refunded), undo a scheduled cancel, and reactivate a cancelled or payment-suspended subscription by paying one month; an operator suspension offers no reactivation.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit (Vitest):
  - [ ] Each prototype state and status-dependent wording (a `TRIALING` tenant with `firstChargePaid` true gets the scheduled wording and with false the immediate one, never a hedge); the already-cancelling and error paths; reactivate → payment-link call sequence; no reactivate action for `OPERATOR`; `BILLING_ADDRESS_REQUIRED` on reactivate → Dados link
  - [ ] The cancel and suspended pages never contain "apagados definitivamente" or "30 e 7 dias" (named test); the summary key is invalidated by each mutation
- E2E:
  - [ ] Covered by S35 — **named here:** `billing-cancel-reactivate.spec.ts`
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S32 — `SubscriptionBanner` and the suspended-manager repoint (UC-130, UC-121)

**Agent:** `frontend-ts`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-130 (all, A1–A6) and UC-121 (manager routing), `docs/DASHBOARD_FRONTEND_ARCHITECTURE.md` § Staff/Manager Shell (there is no single shared layout, but every section layout renders `shells/dashboard/components/DashboardShell.tsx`, which is the mount point), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_SHARED.md` § Tenant access enforcement (item 6 — the suspended redirect lives in the layouts via `loadDashboardShellContext`, never in `proxy.ts`), `journeys/manager/billing.md`
**Dependencies:** M26-S14 (summary fields), M26-S27 (the billing page, the `?tab=` deep links and the shared summary key — S32 repoints to it), M26-S30 (the Plano tab the "Confirmar plano" link opens), M25-S29 (the shared suspended-redirect helper called from `loadDashboardShellContext` / the section layouts — the root `app/dashboard/layout.tsx` is a passthrough and does **not** hold it — plus the `/dashboard/suspended` page and the feature registry)
**Pattern:** One banner component mounted **once** in `shells/dashboard/components/DashboardShell.tsx` — the single component all **eleven** section layouts render (the ten existing ones plus the new `app/dashboard/billing/layout.tsx` of S27), inside the Locale/Tenant providers — above `{children}`; it renders `null` on `/dashboard/billing` and every subpath (decided at the M26 audit: the Resumo already shows its own trial, past-due and address alerts, so a second banner would duplicate them); it reads the shared summary query (`['billing','summary',tenantId]`, with a `staleTime` because it runs on every dashboard navigation); status-driven variants; fail-silent.

**Description:**
**The banner.** `SubscriptionBanner` (manager-only, mounted once above the page content, not dismissible) reads the summary: `TRIALING` → "Faltam N dias do seu teste" linking to billing, with "Confirme seu plano para gerarmos a primeira cobrança" and the **Confirmar plano** call to action until `plan_confirmed_at` is set (trial entitlements stay fully enabled); last days → "sua conta será suspensa se a primeira cobrança não for paga", "Seu teste termina hoje", singular "Falta 1 dia" (A1); `PAST_DUE` → the same component in the amber alert style: "Pagamento em atraso." with the amount, due date, `suspendsAt` and **Pagar agora**, access full and nothing locked, disappearing when the payment is confirmed (A3); billing address missing (`TRIALING` only, UC-130 A4: for a non-trial subscription the address warning is the billing page's and `summary.addressRequired`; BR or US) → an amber banner "Adicione seu endereço de cobrança — sem ele sua conta será suspensa em {fim do teste}" linking to the Dados tab (A4); `ACTIVE`, exempt or load error → no banner (A5, A6 — a load error fails **silent**: no banner, the page is never blocked). **Where it mounts** (decision R9): `DashboardShell.tsx`, the one component every section layout renders (`DashboardSectionShell`, `DashboardLayoutShell` and the hand-rolled bookings/loyalty/schedule layouts all end in it), so the banner appears on the ten non-billing sections with no per-layout edit; `SubscriptionBannerProvider` wraps it there and is exactly one instance. **Banner on the billing section (decided at the M26 audit, 2026-11-18):** the billing section renders the shell too (eleven layouts), but `SubscriptionBanner` returns `null` on `/dashboard/billing` and its subpaths, because Resumo carries the same alerts (prototype dev-notes `01b` / `01c`); so "exactly one banner" holds on the ten other sections and "none" on billing. The banner uses the `ui/notice` primitive S27 adds (variants `info` / `warning`, no ARIA role), never a hand-written `role="alert"`. **"Last days" threshold (decided at the M26 audit, UC-130 A1 already says it):** 3 days or fewer remaining; `banner-state` takes `N = 3` as a named constant. **Precedence:** one banner at a time, by priority `PAST_DUE` (pay) > billing address missing > plan unconfirmed trial > trial countdown (`SUSPENDED` shows no banner: the manager is on the billing page); `banner-state` is table-driven over the combinations. **"Pagar agora" in the banner is a link to `/dashboard/billing`** (it never calls `payment-link`). The summary call is fail-silent. **The repoint.** M25 sent a suspended manager to `/dashboard/suspended` (the notice); UC-121 sends them to the billing page. Change only the target of the M25 shared helper (`suspended-redirect.ts`, called from `loadDashboardShellContext`; `proxy.ts` is **not** modified — it cannot read tenant status, `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_SHARED.md` § Tenant access enforcement, item 6) so a `SUSPENDED` **manager** lands on `/dashboard/billing`, while the staff notice page stays for `STAFF`; the trial-ended-unpaid case lands with "Pague 1 mês para reativar". **Loop guard:** the helper also skips `/dashboard/billing` and every subpath (as it already skips `/dashboard/login` and `/dashboard/suspended`). **Disabled navigation (UC-117 A5, owned here):** while the tenant is `SUSPENDED` the manager's Sidebar, MoreSheet and BottomNav items other than "Assinatura" render `aria-disabled` with no `href` (the prototype draws `href="#"` plus `aria-disabled`; no `href` is the intentional, safer difference), driven by the `operational`/suspended state that **S27 adds to `DashboardShellContext`** (the shell did not receive a suspended flag before: it carried only `lockedFeatures` and `routeFeature`); `resolveDashboardNav` entries gain a `disabled` field this story sets from that state, and the three nav components render it. **`ManagerSuspendedNotice` (decided by editor):** it stays as the page `/dashboard/suspended` renders for a manager who opens that URL directly, but with a link to `/dashboard/billing`; no redirect points to it any more.

**Prototype references:** `journeys/manager/prototypes/billing/04-dashboard-trial-banner.html`, `04b-dashboard-past-due-banner.html`, `01d-billing-suspended.html`, `journeys/manager/billing.md`.
**BFF endpoint spec:** none new — reads `GET /v1/billing/summary` (S14).
**Backend HTTP surface:** none.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** i18n keys under `dashboard.billing.banner.*` in both locales.

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/web/features/billing/components/SubscriptionBanner.tsx`, `SubscriptionBannerProvider.tsx` (+ `.spec.tsx` each) (new)
- `apps/web/features/billing/model/banner-state.ts` (+ spec) (new — pure status → variant mapping incl. day counts in the tenant timezone)
- `apps/web/shells/dashboard/components/DashboardShell.tsx` (+ spec) (modify — mounts `SubscriptionBannerProvider` and `SubscriptionBanner` above `{children}`; the `operational` state it passes to the nav is plumbed by S27)
- `apps/web/shells/dashboard/model/dashboard-nav.ts` (+ `dashboard-nav.spec.ts`) (modify — a `disabled` field on the resolved entries while suspended)
- `apps/web/shells/dashboard/components/Sidebar.tsx`, `MoreSheet.tsx`, `BottomNav.tsx` (+ specs) (modify — render a disabled entry as `aria-disabled` without `href`)
- the eleven section layouts need **no** edit (they all render `DashboardShell`); the spec that renders each lives in `DashboardShell.spec.tsx` / the per-layout specs and is listed in the criteria below
- `apps/web/shared/lib/billing/suspended-redirect.ts` (+ its spec) (modify — uses the `role` input S27 added; the suspended manager's target changes from `/dashboard/suspended` to `/dashboard/billing`; skip `/dashboard/billing` and subpaths; the helper stays called from `loadDashboardShellContext`, never the root `app/dashboard/layout.tsx`)
- `apps/web/features/billing/components/ManagerSuspendedNotice.tsx` (+ spec) (modify — link to the billing page)
- `apps/web/shared/lib/billing/feature-registry.ts` (+ spec) (modify)
- `packages/i18n/locales/pt-BR/web.json`, `packages/i18n/locales/en/web.json` (modify)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A trial manager always sees how many days remain and, until confirmed, the call to confirm the plan; a past-due manager sees the amount, the dates and a pay button, and nothing is locked; an active or exempt manager sees nothing.
- [ ] A suspended manager is taken to the billing page and nowhere else; staff keep the notice.
- [ ] The banner never blocks a page, even if its data fails to load.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit (Vitest):
  - [ ] `banner-state` table-driven (singular/plural, last day, tenant-timezone boundaries, address-missing, exempt); component fails silent on a load error
  - [ ] Suspended-redirect helper: suspended `MANAGER` → billing, `STAFF` → notice, `CUSTOMER` unchanged; **no redirect on `/dashboard/billing` and `/dashboard/billing/cancel`** (loop guard)
  - [ ] Banner on every one of the ten non-billing sections (a spec renders each layout's shell and finds exactly one banner) and **none** on `/dashboard/billing` and its subpaths (including the new billing layout); the banner renders no `role="alert"` / `role="status"` (named test, `ui/notice`); address-missing banner only for `TRIALING`; the last-days threshold is `N = 3`; banner precedence combinations; the banner reuses the shared summary key; suspended manager's other nav items are `aria-disabled` without `href`
- E2E:
  - [ ] Covered by S35 — **named here:** `billing-banner-and-suspension.spec.ts`
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S33 — `/ikaro-trial` public pages, Terms and Privacy hosting (UC-127, UC-128)

**Agent:** `frontend-ts + devops`
**Complexity:** L
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-127 (all, A1–A10) and UC-128, `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md` § Billing — Public Signup API (BFF, no JWT), `docs/HOTSITE_DYNAMIC_ARCHITECTURE.md` (the `/ikaro-trial` routing paragraph), `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § CSP allowances for a new external UI resource and § Hotsite full-page components, `docs/CI_TRAPS.md` § Cloudflare Turnstile's test sitekey, `docs/CODE_STANDARDS.md`, `journeys/guest/ikaro-trial.md`
**Dependencies:** M26-S22 (slug availability), M26-S23 (prices), M26-S24 (session, signup, `termsVersion`, the dev signup-session issuer), M26-S25 (resend), M25-S30 (the `Unavailable` and hotsite shell conventions)
**Pattern:** Public pages branded as the owner tenant (`--ba-*` allowed: this is the hotsite tree), built under `app/[slug]/ikaro-trial/…` for the platform-owner slug only (any other slug gets `notFound()`), reached through the rewrite `/ikaro-trial/*` → `/{PLATFORM_OWNER_TENANT_SLUG}/ikaro-trial/*` (`docs/HOTSITE_DYNAMIC_ARCHITECTURE.md` § The `/ikaro-trial` pages); independent of `isPublished`. The public URLs are `/ikaro-trial`, `/ikaro-trial/entrar`, `/ikaro-trial/conta-criada` (the `nextPath` S24 returns), `/ikaro-trial/termos`, `/ikaro-trial/privacidade`, `/ikaro-trial/conta-nao-verificada` (the unverified-Google-email page S24 redirects to) — **all under the one `ikaro-trial` segment**, so no new top-level segment appears under a tenant slug and the reserved list of S22 needs no new word. Every full-page component paints `backgroundColor: 'var(--ba-background)'` (`docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § Hotsite full-page components). **Locale pin (decided at the M26 audit, 2026-11-18):** the pages inherit locale and currency from the owner tenant's manifest through `app/[slug]/layout`, and the local seed's owner `ikaro` is US/en while the prototypes, the E2E and the legal texts are pt-BR; so the owner tenant must be pt-BR (a stated precondition here, in the S35 fixture and in S39's item 4 when Ikaro's own tenant is created) and the `/ikaro-trial` pages additionally render the legal texts for `pt-BR` explicitly rather than trusting the inherited locale.

**Description:**
The public self-signup flow, following `journeys/guest/prototypes/ikaro-trial/`: **details** with the BR price table (`02`; loading `02c`, error `02d` — signup stays possible, A1 of UC-128; the US variant `02b` is a **documented non-goal until M28**, not a price-list condition: the country selector shows Brasil only, the supported-country rule is S23's `SIGNUP_SUPPORTED_COUNTRIES = ['BR']` (S24 enforces it, `422` for any other country), and the web holds no price-list rule of its own — decided at the M26 audit (2026-11-18) because UC-127 A7 says a missing price list no longer gates the US, and the US price rows are seeded selectable); the **Google step** (`03`; **no Turnstile here** — decision R9: Turnstile is verified only on the final `POST /v1/public/signup/tenant`, so a token never has to survive the full-page Google redirect; the prototype's `03`/`03b` Turnstile parts are superseded, the `03b` "verification failed" screen becomes the failure state of the form step's widget; "trial already used" `03c`: "Entrar na conta existente" is offered **only** when the session read returns `existingTenantSlug` (the same Google `sub`), and — until M27 — the honest "fale com a gente" message instead of discard; **a CPF/CNPJ refusal shows "contact Ikaro" only** (decided at the M26 audit, 2026-11-18: `existingTenantSlug` exists only for the same Google `sub`, and returning another owner's slug for a tax-id match would leak it; UC-127 A3 states the same); the Google button is a link to the BFF signup start that S24 settles); the **form** (`04`, rendered on `/ikaro-trial/entrar` once `useSignupSession` returns a session: business name, slug with live availability check from S22, country (Brasil only), CPF/CNPJ for BR with check digits (**alphanumeric CNPJ accepted**: digits and uppercase letters in the first twelve positions, `^[0-9A-Z]{12}\d{2}$`, input normalised to uppercase — the same rule as S29's identity form, shared through one model file), a **fresh Turnstile widget** (the lead-form `TurnstileWidget`, token single-use, re-requested after every failed submit; it lives in the `platform` slice, so reusing it is a **named cross-slice seam** — `features/billing/components/public/` imports it from `features/platform/` and the import is recorded in the PR; moving it to `shared/` is the alternative and a story-discovery call), the Terms/Privacy checkbox; states `04b` slug taken **with client-generated suggestions** (typed slug plus a suffix, each re-checked through S22's availability call — UC-127 A1), `04c` validation, `04d` submitting, `04e` rate limited with `Retry-After`, `04f` creation failed; and a "cadastro indisponível" notice when the BFF answers `SIGNUP_CLOSED`, decision R7); the **"Google account unverified" error page** (decided at the M26 audit: S33 owns it — S24's callback redirects there when the profile's email-verified flag is false — with copy under `hotsite.ikaroTrial.*`, `noindex`, a retry link back to the Google step and a spec); the **account-created / check-email** page (`05`) with the rate-limited "reenviar e-mail" and, once the first manager exists, a one-click Google sign-in to the existing staff login (A10). Email and name come from the session and are shown read-only; the language derives from the country (no language field). **Terms and Privacy hosting (decision R9):** the backend env `SIGNUP_TERMS_VERSION` is the single source of the version; `GET /v1/public/signup/session` returns `termsVersion`, the form shows the checkbox link to the hosted text for that version and sends the same value back as `acceptedTermsVersion`; **the web has no duplicate version constant**. The owner-supplied legal texts are hosted per version as static content (`features/billing/legal/terms-texts.ts` maps **(locale, version)** → text — the legal texts had no locale dimension, so the key gains one; the Terms are pt-BR only until M28 and the pages pin `pt-BR`; the pages `termos` and `privacidade` render the text for `?v=` or the current version); the story hosts them but **does not write them** (a gate: until the owner supplies the final texts the story ships clearly-marked placeholder pages, `noindex`, that block launch in S39). A spec asserts a hosted text exists for the version the API returns (the dev/CI `SIGNUP_TERMS_VERSION` value is read from the backend's `.env.example`). **Routing and CSP:** the `/ikaro-trial/*` → `/{PLATFORM_OWNER_TENANT_SLUG}/ikaro-trial/*` rewrite is done **in `proxy.ts` at runtime** (a `next.config.ts` rewrite would be baked at build time, and the web container's env is runtime-only); `proxy.ts` sees the original `/ikaro-trial/…` path, which `isHotsiteRoute()` already treats as a hotsite route, so the whole tree **already gets the Turnstile CSP** — `ikaro-trial` must **not** be added to `RESERVED_TOP_SEGMENTS` (a spec asserts every route of the tree gets `challenges.cloudflare.com` in script/frame/connect-src, which also covers the soft navigation from the hotsite CTA). `x-pathname` keeps the original path, so `shared/lib/i18n/resolve-locale.ts` `STATIC_SEGMENTS` gains `ikaro-trial` (otherwise every request fires a manifest lookup for a slug named `ikaro-trial`). **Web env (devops half):** the web needs `PLATFORM_OWNER_TENANT_SLUG` as a server-only runtime env (read in `proxy.ts` for the rewrite and by `getTalkToUsHref()` for "Falar com a gente", S27): add it to `apps/web/.env.example`, to `WEB_REQUIRED_SERVER_ENV_KEYS` in `packages/infra-scripts/src/env-contract.ts`, and to the web `env_vars` in `infra/terraform/envs/staging/main.tf` and `envs/prod/main.tf`; the change is a plain non-secret env var, so it is one app+Terraform PR carrying the `infra-app-mix-ok` label (the playbook row for a plain env var, `infra/terraform/README.md` § New-resource PR-sequencing playbook; confirm the row at story discovery). **Live-verification check:** `gcloud run services describe <web>` shows the variable; `curl -I https://<web>/ikaro-trial` returns the owner tenant's page (200, not 404) and the response CSP contains `challenges.cloudflare.com`; pass = all of it. **SEO:** `/ikaro-trial` is indexable (title/description via `generateMetadata`, canonical `/ikaro-trial` so `/{ownerSlug}/ikaro-trial` is not a duplicate); `/entrar`, `/conta-criada` and the placeholder legal pages return `robots: { index: false, follow: false }` (the lead-form precedent). **Transport:** the public prices fetch uses `bffPublicFetch` in `features/billing/api/public-prices.server.ts`; a client price function `features/billing/api/public-prices.ts` (`bffClient`) backs `usePublicPrices` (the retry of `02d` is a client re-fetch, a server fetcher cannot retry). The session read, slug availability and resend are client hooks through `bffClient` on the same-origin `/v1` gateway (the signup cookie is httpOnly and set on the web origin by the gateway). **Decided at the M26 audit (2026-11-18, one path, matching S24): the final signup `POST /v1/public/signup/tenant` is made server-side**, from a Server Action (or route handler) through `bffServerFetch`-style fetching, **not** through the 8 s client gateway: the Turnstile verification alone can take close to 8 s (S24). The action forwards the incoming `signup_session` cookie to the BFF and relays the BFF's `Set-Cookie` headers (session cleared, resend credential set) and its `Retry-After` and problem-detail body back to the browser; there is no client signup fetcher. The `Retry-After` header of a `429` is surfaced by extending `bffClient`'s error interceptor/`ApiError` with an optional `retryAfterSeconds` (the web has no Retry-After handling today), used by the resend countdown and by the slug-availability throttle; the signup action returns the `Retry-After` seconds in its result object for `04e`. Copy lives under the existing `hotsite.*` namespace (`hotsite.ikaroTrial.*`), not a new top-level namespace; messages use `BrandedFeedbackMessage`/`BrandedFieldFeedback` (`docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § A form's error or success message… Public hotsite). The E2E Google step uses S24's dev/test signup-session issuer (decision R9), not the staff dev-login.

**Prototype references:** `journeys/guest/prototypes/ikaro-trial/01-ikaro-hotsite-cta.html`, `02`–`02d`, `03`–`03c`, `04`–`04g`, `05-welcome-trial-started.html`, `journeys/guest/ikaro-trial.md`.
**BFF endpoint spec:** none new — consumes S22–S25.
**Backend HTTP surface:** none.
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** i18n keys under `hotsite.ikaroTrial.*` in both locales; web server-only env `PLATFORM_OWNER_TENANT_SLUG` (devops half above, `infra-app-mix-ok`); no Terms version constant (the API returns it).
**IAM / permissions:** none.
**Live-verification check:** see the Description (web env var present, `/ikaro-trial` serves the owner page, CSP carries Turnstile).
**PR sequence:** 1 PR (plain env var, app + Terraform together, `infra-app-mix-ok`).

**Files to create/modify:**
- `apps/web/app/[slug]/ikaro-trial/page.tsx`, `apps/web/app/[slug]/ikaro-trial/entrar/page.tsx`, `apps/web/app/[slug]/ikaro-trial/conta-criada/page.tsx` (owner slug only, `notFound()` otherwise) (new — thin; the rewrite mechanism is settled at story discovery)
- `apps/web/features/billing/components/public/IkaroTrialDetails.tsx`, `apps/web/features/billing/components/public/TrialPriceTable.tsx`, `apps/web/features/billing/components/public/TrialGoogleStep.tsx`, `apps/web/features/billing/components/public/TrialSignupForm.tsx`, `apps/web/features/billing/components/public/SlugField.tsx`, `apps/web/features/billing/components/public/AccountCreatedPage.tsx`, `apps/web/features/billing/components/public/TrialAlreadyUsedNotice.tsx` (+ `.spec.tsx` each) (new)
- `apps/web/features/billing/hooks/useSignupSession.ts`, `useSlugAvailability.ts`, `useCreateTenantSignup.ts`, `useResendInvitation.ts` (+ specs), `apps/web/features/billing/model/slug-suggestions.ts` (+ spec) (new)

- `apps/web/features/platform/api/signup.server.ts` (+ spec) (new — the **server-side** final signup `POST` (Server Action) through `bffServerFetch`-style fetching: forwards the `signup_session` cookie, relays `Set-Cookie`, `Retry-After` and the problem body; decided at the audit, S24 settled the same path)
- `apps/web/app/[slug]/ikaro-trial/conta-nao-verificada/page.tsx` (new — thin; the "Google account unverified" page, `noindex`) and `apps/web/features/billing/components/public/GoogleAccountUnverifiedNotice.tsx` (+ `.spec.tsx`) (new)
- `apps/web/features/billing/model/tax-id.ts` (+ spec) (new — the CPF / alphanumeric-CNPJ check-digit rules shared by this form and S29's `identity-form.ts`)

- `apps/web/app/[slug]/ikaro-trial/termos/page.tsx`, `apps/web/app/[slug]/ikaro-trial/privacidade/page.tsx`, `apps/web/features/billing/legal/terms-texts.ts` (+ spec) (new)
- `apps/web/proxy.ts` (+ `proxy.spec.ts`) (modify — the runtime rewrite `/ikaro-trial/*` → `/{PLATFORM_OWNER_TENANT_SLUG}/ikaro-trial/*`; no CSP change, the tree is already a hotsite route)

**Acceptance criteria — product:**
- [ ] A visitor can read the features and Brazilian prices, pass Turnstile, sign in with Google, choose a free slug, enter CPF/CNPJ, accept the Terms and create a trial account, then be told to check their email — with a clear, specific message for every failure (taken slug, invalid tax id, rate limit, trial already used, creation failure).
- [ ] The US option is not offered; the Terms and Privacy pages are reachable from the checkbox and their version is recorded.

**Acceptance criteria — technical:**
- Unit (Vitest):
  - [ ] The final signup `POST` is made by the server action (no client signup fetcher exists); it forwards the session cookie and relays `Set-Cookie` and `Retry-After`; the unverified-Google-email page renders; a CPF/CNPJ refusal shows "contact Ikaro" only and "Entrar na conta existente" appears only with `existingTenantSlug`; the terms text is resolved by `(locale, version)`; an alphanumeric CNPJ passes the check digits
  - [ ] Each prototype state renders; slug availability debounced; CPF/CNPJ check digits; form submits no email/name and sends back the API's `termsVersion`; a fresh Turnstile token is requested after each failed submit
  - [ ] `proxy.spec.ts`: every route of the `/ikaro-trial` tree gets the Turnstile CSP and is rewritten to the owner slug; `RESERVED_TOP_SEGMENTS` is unchanged; every full-page component paints `var(--ba-background)`
  - [ ] A hosted legal text exists for the `termsVersion` the API returns; `SIGNUP_CLOSED` renders the notice; `ApiError.retryAfterSeconds` is read from `Retry-After` and drives `04e`; slug suggestions are re-checked through the availability call; form errors use `BrandedFeedbackMessage`
  - [ ] `/entrar` and `/conta-criada` set `noindex`; `/ikaro-trial` sets the canonical
- E2E:
  - [ ] Covered by S35 — **named here:** `ikaro-trial-signup.spec.ts` (Turnstile test sitekey: wait on the hidden `cf-turnstile-response` input's value, not an iframe; the Google step via S24's dev/test signup-session issuer) and a soft-navigation spec proving the Turnstile widget loads when arriving from the hotsite CTA
- [ ] Coverage ≥80% on changed code
- [ ] `tsc --noEmit` clean, lint clean

### M26-S34 — `TRIAL_SIGNUP_CTA` component and editor panel (UC-131)

**Agent:** `frontend-ts`
**Complexity:** M
**Docs to load:** `docs/USE_CASES.md` UC-131 (all), `docs/HOTSITE_DYNAMIC_ARCHITECTURE.md` § 3, § 4 `TRIAL_SIGNUP_CTA` and § Adding a New Module, `docs/ENGINEERING_RULES_FRONTEND.md` § Hotsite full-page components and § CSP allowances for a new external UI resource, `docs/DASHBOARD_FRONTEND_ARCHITECTURE.md`, `journeys/manager/hotsite.md` (the appended Billing section), `journeys/manager/prototypes/hotsite/dev-notes.md`
**Dependencies:** M26-S26 (the module contract, the owner rule, `isPlatformOwner` on the hotsite admin GET and the minimal web registration), M26-S33 (the `/ikaro-trial` target exists)
**Pattern:** Registry-driven module addition mirroring `BOOKING_CTA` (public component + editor config panel + lazy registry + module schema), owner-only offering in the editor.

**Description:**
The public component and the editor panel for the 13th module. **Public:** a call to action that renders `title`, optional `subtitle`, a button with `ctaLabel` and the fixed target `/ikaro-trial` (the target is not configurable), with the `centered` / `left-aligned` variants (default label "Testar grátis por 14 dias"), rendered as a server component **through `HotsiteSectionShell`** (which owns the section surface, so the module does **not** paint `--ba-background` itself — that rule is for full-page views, and painting it would override `presentation.surface`), using `sectionTextColor` / `sectionCardBackground` and `var(--ba-*)` only (`docs/HOTSITE_DYNAMIC_ARCHITECTURE.md` § 7. Adding a New Module, step 2); the link is a `next/link` to `/ikaro-trial`; disabled → not rendered, `/ikaro-trial` still works if linked (A3). **Editor:** a `TrialSignupCtaConfigPanel` in `apps/web/features/platform/components/hotsite/modules/` (title, text, button label, variant, presentation), registered in the lazy-panel registry; **offered only to the platform-owner tenant's manager** — the editor reads `isPlatformOwner` from the hotsite admin `GET` response that S26 adds (decision R9), with the backend rule of S26 as the enforcement (A1) and the editor only a convenience. **Owner-aware registry (S26 registers the type minimally so `tsc` stays green; this story completes it):** `MODULE_ORDER`/`materializeLayout` (`features/platform/hotsite/default-layout.ts`) and `manifest-schema.ts` (`MODULE_TYPE_SET`, `tooManyModules`) take the owner flag and omit `TRIAL_SIGNUP_CTA` for every other tenant, so a non-owner's layout list still shows twelve rows and its saves never carry the type (a spec per non-owner path); `DEFAULT_MODULE_DATA` gains an entry. **File discovery follows the §7 checklist** (every exhaustive `Record<HotsiteModuleType, …>` and switch), not a grep of `BOOKING_CTA`: that includes `LayoutTab.tsx`, `ModuleConfigPreviewScreen.tsx`, `hotsite-module-buffer.ts`, `hotsite-template-application.ts`, `HotsiteEditor.tsx` and the `dashboard.hotsitePage.layout.modules.TRIAL_SIGNUP_CTA` label (S26 registers the key; this story only checks it renders in the Layout list). Validation errors (empty label, title too long) show inline (A2). Section background is the section-level `presentation.surface`, not a data field. The editor/panel states are in the prototype's appended GAP screens (`01g`, `01h`). No CSP change is needed: `/ikaro-trial` is already a hotsite route with the Turnstile allowance (S33); this story adds a spec that the hotsite → `/ikaro-trial` soft navigation keeps Turnstile working.

**Prototype references:** `journeys/manager/prototypes/hotsite/01g-module-config-trial-signup-cta.html`, `01h-module-config-trial-signup-cta-erro.html`, `journeys/manager/hotsite.md`.
**BFF endpoint spec:** none new — saves through the existing `PATCH /v1/tenants/hotsite`.
**Backend HTTP surface:** none new (S26).
**New migration / i18n keys / env vars / feature flags:** i18n keys under the hotsite module copy in both locales.

- `apps/web/features/platform/components/hotsite/modules/TrialSignupCtaConfigPanel.tsx` (+ `.spec.tsx`) (new)
- `apps/web/shells/hotsite/components/HotsiteModuleView.tsx` (modify — render the new type)
- `apps/web/shells/hotsite/components/TrialSignupCtaModule.tsx` (+ `.spec.tsx`) (new — named `XxxModule.tsx` per the §7 checklist)
- `apps/web/features/platform/components/hotsite/hotsite-editor-lazy-panels.tsx` (+ spec) (modify)
- `apps/web/features/platform/hotsite/module-schemas.ts`, `module-presentation.ts`, `render-plan.types.ts`, `page-model.ts`, `default-layout.ts`, `presentation-tokens.ts`, `manifest-schema.ts` (+ specs) (modify — owner-aware `MODULE_ORDER`/`materializeLayout`/`manifest-schema`; complete the entries S26 registered minimally)
- `apps/web/features/platform/components/hotsite/LayoutTab.tsx`, `ModuleConfigPreviewScreen.tsx`, `hotsite-module-buffer.ts`, `hotsite-template-application.ts`, `HotsiteEditor.tsx`, `HotsiteEditorMainView.tsx` (+ specs) (modify — only where the §7 checklist finds an exhaustive record or switch over `HotsiteModuleType`; the owner flag reaches the editor from the admin GET)
